<script>
/* ======================================================== helpers */
const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);
const esc = s => String(s ?? "").replace(/[&<>"]/g, c => ({"&": "&amp;", "<": "&lt;", ">": "&gt;", '"': "&quot;"}[c]));
const css = name => getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue(name).trim();
function fmt(b, d = 1) { const u = ["B", "KB", "MB", "GB", "TB"]; let i = 0; b = b || 0; while (b >= 1024 && i < 4) { b /= 1024; i++; } return b.toFixed(i ? d : 0) + " " + u[i]; }
function rate(bps) { const b = (bps || 0) * 8; return b >= 1e9 ? (b / 1e9).toFixed(2) + " Gbit/s" : b >= 1e6 ? (b / 1e6).toFixed(1) + " Mbit/s" : b >= 1e3 ? (b / 1e3).toFixed(0) + " kbit/s" : b.toFixed(0) + " bit/s"; }
function rateParts(bps) { const [n, u] = rate(bps).split(" "); return `${n}<small>${u}</small>`; }
function uptime(s) { const d = Math.floor(s / 86400), h = Math.floor(s % 86400 / 3600), m = Math.floor(s % 3600 / 60); return (d ? d + "d " : "") + h + "h " + m + "m"; }
function ago(t) { if (!t) return "–"; const s = Date.now() / 1000 - t; return s < 5 ? "now" : s < 60 ? Math.round(s) + "s ago" : s < 3600 ? Math.round(s / 60) + " min ago" : Math.round(s / 3600) + " h ago"; }
const level = p => p >= 85 ? "bad" : p >= 60 ? "warn" : "accent";
const sigLevel = s => s >= 55 ? "good" : s >= 35 ? "warn" : "bad";
const tLevel = t => t >= 80 ? "bad" : t >= 60 ? "warn" : "good";
const cv = l => `var(--${l})`;
const bar = (p, l = "accent") => `<div class="bar"><span style="width:${Math.max(0, Math.min(100, p))}%;--c:${cv(l)}"></span></div>`;
async function api(path) { const r = await fetch(path); const d = await r.json(); if (!r.ok) throw new Error(d.error || "request failed"); return d; }
const banner = (l, text) => `<div class="banner" style="--c:${cv(l)};--c-soft:var(--${l === "accent" ? "accent" : l}-soft)">${text}</div>`;
const kpi = (icon, l, k, v, e, go) => `<div class="kpi${go ? " click" : ""}" ${go ? `data-go="${go}"` : ""} style="--c:${cv(l)};--c-soft:var(--${l}-soft)"><div class="k"><i>${ICON[icon]}</i>${k}</div><div class="v">${v}</div><div class="e">${e || "&nbsp;"}</div></div>`;
const PALETTE = ["accent", "teal", "violet", "warn", "bad", "good"];
function avatar(name) { let h = 0; for (const c of name) h = (h * 31 + c.charCodeAt(0)) >>> 0; return `<div class="ava" style="background:var(--${PALETTE[h % PALETTE.length]})">${esc((name[0] || "?").toUpperCase())}</div>`; }

const I = p => `<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round">${p}</svg>`;
const ICON = {
  overview: I('<rect x="3" y="3" width="7" height="9" rx="2"/><rect x="14" y="3" width="7" height="5" rx="2"/><rect x="14" y="12" width="7" height="9" rx="2"/><rect x="3" y="16" width="7" height="5" rx="2"/>'),
  system: I('<rect x="5" y="5" width="14" height="14" rx="2"/><rect x="9" y="9" width="6" height="6" rx="1"/><path d="M9 2v3M15 2v3M9 19v3M15 19v3M2 9h3M2 15h3M19 9h3M19 15h3"/>'),
  disk: I('<ellipse cx="12" cy="5" rx="8" ry="3"/><path d="M4 5v14c0 1.7 3.6 3 8 3s8-1.3 8-3V5M4 12c0 1.7 3.6 3 8 3s8-1.3 8-3"/>'),
  wifi: I('<path d="M2 8.8a15 15 0 0 1 20 0M5 12.5a10 10 0 0 1 14 0M8.5 16a5 5 0 0 1 7 0"/><circle cx="12" cy="19.5" r="1" fill="currentColor"/>'),
  bt: I('<path d="M7 7l10 10-5 5V2l5 5L7 17"/>'),
  lan: I('<rect x="9" y="2" width="6" height="6" rx="1"/><rect x="2" y="16" width="6" height="6" rx="1"/><rect x="16" y="16" width="6" height="6" rx="1"/><path d="M12 8v4M5 16v-4h14v4"/>'),
  ports: I('<rect x="3" y="11" width="18" height="10" rx="2"/><path d="M7 11V7a5 5 0 0 1 10 0v4"/>'),
  traffic: I('<path d="M7 17V5M3 9l4-4 4 4M17 7v12M13 15l4 4 4-4"/>'),
  down: I('<path d="M12 4v14M6 12l6 6 6-6"/>'), up: I('<path d="M12 20V6M6 12l6-6 6 6"/>'),
  link: I('<path d="M10 14a5 5 0 0 0 7 0l3-3a5 5 0 0 0-7-7l-1 1M14 10a5 5 0 0 0-7 0l-3 3a5 5 0 0 0 7 7l1-1"/>'),
  ram: I('<rect x="2" y="7" width="20" height="10" rx="2"/><path d="M6 11v2M10 11v2M14 11v2M18 11v2"/>'),
  temp: I('<path d="M14 14.8V4a2 2 0 0 0-4 0v10.8a4 4 0 1 0 4 0z"/>'),
  clock: I('<circle cx="12" cy="12" r="9"/><path d="M12 7v5l3 2"/>'),
  globe: I('<circle cx="12" cy="12" r="9"/><path d="M3 12h18M12 3a14 14 0 0 1 0 18M12 3a14 14 0 0 0 0 18"/>'),
  ear: I('<path d="M6 8.5a6 6 0 1 1 12 0c0 4-4 4.5-4 8a3 3 0 0 1-6 0"/><path d="M9.5 9a2.5 2.5 0 0 1 5 0"/>'),
  shield: I('<path d="M12 3l8 3v6c0 5-3.5 8-8 9-4.5-1-8-4-8-9V6z"/>'),
};

/* ======================================================== theme */
function setTheme(t, save) {
  document.documentElement.dataset.theme = t;
  document.querySelectorAll(".theme button").forEach(b => b.classList.toggle("on", b.dataset.t === t));
  if (save) try { localStorage.setItem("cs-theme", t); } catch (e) {}
  redrawCharts();
}
document.querySelectorAll(".theme button").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => setTheme(b.dataset.t, true));
matchMedia("(prefers-color-scheme: dark)").addEventListener("change", e => {
  let saved = null; try { saved = localStorage.getItem("cs-theme"); } catch (er) {}
  if (!saved) setTheme(e.matches ? "dark" : "light", false);
});

/* ======================================================== charts */
function hexA(hex, a) { const n = parseInt(hex.slice(1), 16); return `rgba(${n >> 16 & 255},${n >> 8 & 255},${n & 255},${a})`; }
const charts = {};
function lineChart(id, series, { max, n = 60, label = v => v } = {}) {
  charts[id] = [series, { max, n, label }];
  const c = $(id); if (!c || !c.offsetParent) return;
  const dpr = devicePixelRatio || 1, W = c.clientWidth, H = c.clientHeight;
  if (!W) return;
  c.width = W * dpr; c.height = H * dpr;
  const g = c.getContext("2d"); g.scale(dpr, dpr);
  const top = 14, bottom = H - 6, left = 0, right = W;
  const mx = max || Math.max(1, ...series.flatMap(s => s.data));
  g.strokeStyle = css("--border"); g.lineWidth = 1; g.setLineDash([3, 4]);
  for (let i = 0; i <= 3; i++) { const y = Math.round(top + (bottom - top) * i / 3) + .5; g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(left, y); g.lineTo(right, y); g.stroke(); }
  g.setLineDash([]);
  g.fillStyle = css("--faint"); g.font = "11px Inter, sans-serif"; g.textAlign = "right";
  g.fillText(label(mx), right - 2, top - 3);
  series.forEach(({ data, color }) => {
    const col = css(color); if (data.length < 2) return;
    const step = (right - left) / (n - 1);
    const pts = data.map((v, i) => [right - (data.length - 1 - i) * step, bottom - Math.min(1, v / mx) * (bottom - top)]);
    g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(pts[0][0], pts[0][1]);
    for (let i = 1; i < pts.length; i++) { const [x0, y0] = pts[i - 1], [x1, y1] = pts[i], xm = (x0 + x1) / 2; g.bezierCurveTo(xm, y0, xm, y1, x1, y1); }
    g.strokeStyle = col; g.lineWidth = 2.2; g.lineJoin = "round"; g.stroke();
    g.lineTo(pts[pts.length - 1][0], bottom); g.lineTo(pts[0][0], bottom); g.closePath();
    const grad = g.createLinearGradient(0, top, 0, bottom); grad.addColorStop(0, hexA(col, .22)); grad.addColorStop(1, hexA(col, 0));
    g.fillStyle = grad; g.fill();
    const [lx, ly] = pts[pts.length - 1];
    g.beginPath(); g.arc(lx, ly, 3.5, 0, 7); g.fillStyle = col; g.fill();
    g.beginPath(); g.arc(lx, ly, 7, 0, 7); g.fillStyle = hexA(col, .18); g.fill();
  });
}
function redrawCharts() { for (const id in charts) lineChart(id, ...charts[id]); }
addEventListener("resize", redrawCharts);

function gauge(el, pct, label, big, sub, l) {
  const r = 52, c = 2 * Math.PI * r, arc = c * .75, val = arc * Math.min(100, Math.max(0, pct)) / 100;
  el.innerHTML = `<svg viewBox="0 0 140 124">
    <circle cx="70" cy="70" r="${r}" fill="none" stroke="var(--track)" stroke-width="11" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-dasharray="${arc} ${c}" transform="rotate(135 70 70)"/>
    <circle cx="70" cy="70" r="${r}" fill="none" stroke="${cv(l)}" stroke-width="11" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-dasharray="${val} ${c}" transform="rotate(135 70 70)" style="transition:stroke-dasharray .6s"/>
    <text x="70" y="76" text-anchor="middle" font-family="Inter, sans-serif" font-size="25" font-weight="800" fill="var(--text)">${big}</text></svg>
    <div class="lbl">${label}</div><div class="sub">${esc(sub)}</div>`;
}
function donut(parts) {
  const total = parts.reduce((s, p) => s + p.v, 0);
  if (!total) return `<div class="msg">no connections right now</div>`;
  const r = 46, c = 2 * Math.PI * r; let off = 0;
  const rings = parts.filter(p => p.v).map(p => { const len = p.v / total * c; const s = `<circle cx="60" cy="60" r="${r}" fill="none" stroke="${cv(p.l)}" stroke-width="16" stroke-dasharray="${len} ${c - len}" stroke-dashoffset="${-off}" transform="rotate(-90 60 60)"/>`; off += len; return s; }).join("");
  return `<div style="display:flex;gap:18px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap">
    <svg viewBox="0 0 120 120" style="width:150px;flex:none"><circle cx="60" cy="60" r="${r}" fill="none" stroke="var(--track)" stroke-width="16"/>${rings}
      <text x="60" y="60" text-anchor="middle" font-size="24" font-weight="800" fill="var(--text)" font-family="Inter">${total}</text>
      <text x="60" y="77" text-anchor="middle" font-size="10" fill="var(--muted)" font-family="Inter">connections</text></svg>
    <div style="flex:1;min-width:140px">${parts.map(p => `<div class="brow" style="grid-template-columns:14px 1fr auto"><i style="width:10px;height:10px;border-radius:3px;background:${cv(p.l)}"></i><span>${p.k}</span><b>${p.v}</b></div>`).join("")}</div></div>`;
}

/* ======================================================== navigation */
const VIEWS = [
  ["overview", "Overview", "Everything at a glance"], ["system", "System", "Processor, memory, storage and processes"],
  ["disk", "Disk", "Drives and what is taking up the space"], ["wifi", "Wi-Fi", "Wireless networks around you"],
  ["bt", "Bluetooth", "Bluetooth devices nearby"], ["lan", "Network", "Your wired connection and home network"],
  ["ports", "Ports", "Check which services a device exposes"], ["traffic", "Traffic", "Live incoming and outgoing traffic of your programs"],
];
$("nav").innerHTML = VIEWS.map(([v, name], i) => `<button data-v="${v}">${ICON[v]}<span>${name}</span><kbd>${i + 1}</kbd></button>`).join("");
let view = "overview";
const onShow = {};
function show(v) {
  view = v;
  const meta = VIEWS.find(x => x[0] === v);
  $("title").textContent = meta[1]; $("subtitle").textContent = meta[2];
  document.querySelectorAll("#nav button").forEach(b => b.classList.toggle("on", b.dataset.v === v));
  document.querySelectorAll(".view").forEach(s => s.hidden = s.id !== "v-" + v);
  try { localStorage.setItem("cs-view", v); } catch (e) {}
  history.replaceState(null, "", "#" + v);
  if (onShow[v]) onShow[v]();
  requestAnimationFrame(redrawCharts);
}
$("nav").onclick = e => { const b = e.target.closest("button"); if (b) show(b.dataset.v); };
document.addEventListener("click", e => { const g = e.target.closest("[data-go]"); if (g) show(g.dataset.go); });
addEventListener("keydown", e => {
  if (e.target.tagName === "INPUT" || e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey || e.altKey) return;
  const v = VIEWS[+e.key - 1]; if (v) show(v[0]);
});
setInterval(() => { $("clock").textContent = new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit", second: "2-digit" }); }, 500);

/* shared state for the overview */
const S = { sys: null, traffic: null, wifi: null, bt: null, lan: null };

/* ======================================================== SYSTEM */
const hist = { cpu: [], ram: [], io: [] };
let sortBy = "cpu";
$("procSort").onclick = e => { const b = e.target.closest("button"); if (!b) return; sortBy = b.dataset.s; $("procSort").querySelectorAll("button").forEach(x => x.classList.toggle("on", x === b)); renderSys(); };
function rootFs(d) { return d.storage.find(s => s.mounts.includes("/")) || d.storage[0] || { used: 0, total: 1, free: 0 }; }
function cpuTemp(d) { return d.temps.find(x => /package|tctl|tdie/i.test(x.label)) || d.temps.find(x => x.sensor === "coretemp" || x.sensor === "k10temp") || d.temps[0]; }
function renderSys() {
  const d = S.sys; if (!d) return;
  const i = d.info, c = d.cpu, m = d.mem;
  $("host").textContent = i.hostname; $("hostSub").textContent = `${i.os} · up ${uptime(d.uptime)}`;
  if (view !== "system") return;
  $("cpuModel").textContent = `${i.cpu_model} · ${i.cores} threads`;
  const ramPct = m.total ? m.used / m.total * 100 : 0, root = rootFs(d), romPct = root.used / root.total * 100, t = cpuTemp(d);
  gauge($("gCpu"), c.total, "Processor", Math.round(c.total) + "%", "load " + c.load[0].toFixed(2), level(c.total));
  gauge($("gRam"), ramPct, "Memory", Math.round(ramPct) + "%", `${fmt(m.used)} of ${fmt(m.total)}`, level(ramPct) === "accent" ? "violet" : level(ramPct));
  gauge($("gRom"), romPct, "Main drive", Math.round(romPct) + "%", fmt(root.free) + " free", level(romPct) === "accent" ? "teal" : level(romPct));
  if (t) gauge($("gTemp"), t.c, "Temperature", Math.round(t.c) + "°", "CPU, celsius", tLevel(t.c)); else gauge($("gTemp"), 0, "Temperature", "n/a", "no sensor found", "muted");

  $("cores").innerHTML = c.cores.map((p, n) => `<div class="brow"><span class="k">Core ${n}</span>${bar(p, level(p))}<span class="v">${p.toFixed(0)}%</span></div>`).join("");
  const fr = c.freqs.filter(Boolean);
  $("cpuKv").innerHTML = [["Load (1/5/15 min)", c.load.map(x => x.toFixed(2)).join(" · ")], ["Speed now", fr.length ? Math.round(fr.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / fr.length) + " MHz" : "n/a"],
    ["Max speed", c.max_freq ? c.max_freq + " MHz" : "n/a"], ["Tasks running / total", c.tasks], ["Threads", i.cores], ["Kernel", i.kernel]]
    .map(([k, v]) => `<div><div class="k">${k}</div><div class="v">${esc(v)}</div></div>`).join("");

  const swapPct = m.swap_total ? m.swap_used / m.swap_total * 100 : 0;
  $("memTotal").textContent = fmt(m.total) + " installed";
  $("mem").innerHTML = [["In use", ramPct, fmt(m.used), "violet"], ["Cache", m.cache / m.total * 100, fmt(m.cache), "teal"], ["Swap", swapPct, m.swap_total ? fmt(m.swap_used) : "none", level(swapPct)]]
    .map(([k, p, v, l]) => `<div class="brow"><span class="k">${k}</span>${bar(p, l)}<span class="v">${v}</span></div>`).join("");
  $("memKv").innerHTML = [["Available", fmt(m.available)], ["Free (idle)", fmt(m.free)], ["Swap size", fmt(m.swap_total)], ["Processes", d.procs.count]]
    .map(([k, v]) => `<div><div class="k">${k}</div><div class="v">${esc(v)}</div></div>`).join("");

  $("storage").innerHTML = d.storage.map(s => { const p = s.used / s.total * 100;
    return `<div class="brow" title="${esc(s.device)} · ${esc(s.fstype)}"><span class="k">${esc(s.mounts[0])}</span>${bar(p, level(p) === "accent" ? "teal" : level(p))}<span class="v">${p.toFixed(0)}%</span></div>
      <div class="faint" style="font-size:12px;margin:-3px 0 6px 88px">${fmt(s.used)} of ${fmt(s.total)} · ${esc(s.fstype)}</div>`; }).join("");
  $("ioKv").innerHTML = d.io.map(x => `<div><div class="k">${esc(x.disk)} · ${x.ssd ? "SSD" : "HDD"} · ${fmt(x.size, 0)}</div><div class="v" style="font-size:13.5px">↓ ${fmt(x.read)}/s · ↑ ${fmt(x.write)}/s</div></div>`).join("");

  const list = sortBy === "cpu" ? d.procs.by_cpu : d.procs.by_mem;
  $("procs").innerHTML = `<thead><tr><th>Program</th><th class="n">PID</th><th>User</th><th class="n">CPU</th><th class="n">Memory</th><th class="n">RAM</th></tr></thead><tbody>` +
    list.map(p => `<tr><td class="strong">${esc(p.name)}</td><td class="n muted">${p.pid}</td><td class="muted">${esc(p.user)}</td>
      <td class="n"><span class="pill ${p.cpu >= 50 ? "p-bad" : p.cpu >= 10 ? "p-warn" : "p-mute"}">${p.cpu.toFixed(1)}%</span></td><td class="n">${p.mem_pct.toFixed(1)}%</td><td class="n">${fmt(p.mem)}</td></tr>`).join("") + "</tbody>";

  $("temps").innerHTML = d.temps.length ? d.temps.map(t => `<div class="brow"><span class="k" title="${esc(t.label)}">${esc(t.label.replace("Package id 0", "Package"))}</span>${bar(t.c, tLevel(t.c))}<span class="v">${t.c.toFixed(0)}°C</span></div>`).join("") : `<div class="msg">no temperature sensors found</div>`;
  $("battery").innerHTML = d.battery ? `Battery <b>${d.battery.percent}%</b> · ${esc(d.battery.status.toLowerCase())}` : "";
  lineChart("sysChart", [{ data: hist.io.map(v => v / Math.max(1, ...hist.io) * 100), color: "--teal" }, { data: hist.ram, color: "--violet" }, { data: hist.cpu, color: "--accent" }], { max: 100, label: v => v + "%" });
}
let fails = 0;
async function sysTick() {
  try {
    const d = await api("/api/sys/stats"); if (!d.cpu) return;
    hist.cpu.push(d.cpu.total); hist.ram.push(d.mem.total ? d.mem.used / d.mem.total * 100 : 0); hist.io.push(d.io.reduce((s, x) => s + x.read + x.write, 0));
    for (const k in hist) if (hist[k].length > 60) hist[k].shift();
    S.sys = d; renderSys(); renderOverview();
    fails = 0; $("live").className = "live"; $("live").innerHTML = "<i></i> Live";
  } catch (e) { if (++fails > 2) { $("live").className = "live off"; $("live").innerHTML = "<i></i> Offline – is collective.py running?"; } }
}
onShow.system = renderSys;

/* ======================================================== TRAFFIC */
let tDir = "all";
$("tDir").onclick = e => { const b = e.target.closest("button"); if (!b) return; tDir = b.dataset.d; $("tDir").querySelectorAll("button").forEach(x => x.classList.toggle("on", x === b)); renderTraffic(); };
$("tSearch").oninput = () => renderTraffic();
$("tLocal").onchange = () => renderTraffic();
function appRows(apps, limit) {
  const list = apps.filter(a => a.app !== "unknown" || a.sent + a.recv > 0).slice(0, limit);
  if (!list.length) return `<div class="msg">No program has used the network yet.</div>`;
  const max = Math.max(1, ...list.map(a => a.sent + a.recv));
  return list.map(a => { const tot = a.sent + a.recv, w = tot / max * 100;
    return `<div class="prog">${avatar(a.app)}<div style="min-width:0"><div class="nm">${esc(a.app)}</div>
      <div class="ds">${a.connections ? `${a.connections} open · ${esc(a.remotes.slice(0, 3).join(", "))}` : `idle · last active ${ago(a.last)}`}</div>
      <div class="dbar" style="width:${Math.max(4, w)}%"><span style="width:${tot ? a.recv / tot * 100 : 50}%;background:var(--accent)"></span><span style="flex:1;background:var(--teal)"></span></div></div>
      <div class="tt"><div><span class="muted">↓</span> <b>${fmt(a.recv)}</b></div><div><span class="muted">↑</span> <b>${fmt(a.sent)}</b></div>
      ${a.in_rate + a.out_rate > 100 ? `<div class="pill p-good" style="margin:2px 0 0">${rate(a.in_rate + a.out_rate)}</div>` : ""}</div></div>`; }).join("");
}
function renderTraffic() {
  const d = S.traffic; if (!d || view !== "traffic") return;
  const net = d.connections.filter(c => c.kind !== "this computer");
  const last = d.user_hist[d.user_hist.length - 1] || { rx: 0, tx: 0 }, all = d.iface_hist[d.iface_hist.length - 1] || { rx: 0, tx: 0 };
  const mins = Math.max(1, Math.round((d.time - d.started) / 60));
  $("tUser").textContent = d.user;
  $("tKpis").innerHTML = [
    kpi("down", "accent", "Your download", rateParts(last.rx), "your programs, right now"),
    kpi("up", "teal", "Your upload", rateParts(last.tx), "your programs, right now"),
    kpi("link", "violet", "Open connections", net.length, `${net.filter(c => c.direction === "out").length} outgoing · ${net.filter(c => c.direction === "in").length} incoming`),
    kpi("globe", "warn", "Data this session", fmt(d.session.recv + d.session.sent), `↓ ${fmt(d.session.recv)} · ↑ ${fmt(d.session.sent)} · ${mins} min`),
    kpi("ear", "good", "Listening ports", d.listening.length, `${d.listening.filter(l => l.open_to === "the network").length} open to the network`),
  ].join("");
  $("tIfaces").textContent = Object.keys(d.ifaces).join(", ") + ` · ↓ ${rate(all.rx)} · ↑ ${rate(all.tx)}`;
  lineChart("tUserChart", [{ data: d.user_hist.map(x => x.tx), color: "--teal" }, { data: d.user_hist.map(x => x.rx), color: "--accent" }], { n: 60, label: rate });
  lineChart("tAllChart", [{ data: d.iface_hist.map(x => x.tx), color: "--teal" }, { data: d.iface_hist.map(x => x.rx), color: "--accent" }], { n: 120, label: rate });
  $("tApps").innerHTML = appRows(d.apps, 8);
  const cnt = k => net.filter(c => c.kind === k).length;
  $("tDonut").innerHTML = donut([{ k: "Internet", v: cnt("internet"), l: "accent" }, { k: "Home network", v: cnt("home network"), l: "teal" }, { k: "Broadcast / other", v: net.length - cnt("internet") - cnt("home network"), l: "violet" }])
    + `<div class="note">${d.other_connections} more connection${d.other_connections === 1 ? "" : "s"} belong to the system or other users and are not shown.</div>`;

  const q = $("tSearch").value.trim().toLowerCase();
  let list = $("tLocal").checked ? d.connections : net;
  if (tDir !== "all") list = list.filter(c => c.direction === tDir);
  if (q) list = list.filter(c => [c.app, c.remote, c.name, c.rport, c.lport, c.service, c.proto].join(" ").toLowerCase().includes(q));
  list = [...list].sort((a, b) => (b.in_rate + b.out_rate) - (a.in_rate + a.out_rate) || (b.recv + b.sent) - (a.recv + a.sent));
  $("tCount").textContent = `${list.length} shown`;
  const kindPill = k => `<span class="pill ${k === "internet" ? "" : k === "home network" ? "p-teal" : "p-mute"}">${esc(k)}</span>`;
  $("tConns").innerHTML = `<thead><tr><th></th><th>Program</th><th>Remote</th><th>Service</th><th>Where</th><th class="n">Received</th><th class="n">Sent</th><th class="n">Speed</th><th>State</th></tr></thead><tbody>` +
    (list.map(c => { const sp = c.in_rate + c.out_rate, tcp = c.proto === "tcp";
      return `<tr><td><span class="arrow ${c.direction}" title="${c.direction === "in" ? "incoming" : "outgoing"}">${c.direction === "in" ? "↓" : "↑"}</span></td>
      <td class="strong">${esc(c.app)} <span class="faint" style="font-weight:400">${c.pid ?? ""}</span></td>
      <td><div>${esc(c.name || c.remote)}</div><div class="mono faint">${esc(c.remote)}:${c.rport}</div></td>
      <td>${esc(c.service || "port " + (c.direction === "in" ? c.lport : c.rport))} <span class="faint">${c.proto.toUpperCase()}</span></td>
      <td>${kindPill(c.kind)}</td>
      <td class="n">${tcp ? fmt(c.recv) : '<span class="faint">–</span>'}</td><td class="n">${tcp ? fmt(c.sent) : '<span class="faint">–</span>'}</td>
      <td class="n">${sp > 50 ? `<span class="pill p-good">${rate(sp)}</span>` : '<span class="faint">idle</span>'}</td>
      <td class="muted">${esc(c.state.toLowerCase())}</td></tr>`; }).join("") || `<tr><td colspan="9" class="msg">no connections match</td></tr>`) + "</tbody>";

  $("tListen").innerHTML = `<thead><tr><th>Program</th><th>Port</th><th>Service</th><th>Reachable from</th><th>Address</th></tr></thead><tbody>` +
    (d.listening.map(l => `<tr><td class="strong">${esc(l.app || "unknown")}</td><td class="mono">${l.port} <span class="faint">${l.proto.toUpperCase()}</span></td><td>${esc(l.service || "–")}</td>
      <td>${l.open_to === "the network" ? '<span class="pill p-warn">the network</span>' : '<span class="pill p-good">only this computer</span>'}</td><td class="mono faint">${esc(l.ip)}</td></tr>`).join("")
      || `<tr><td colspan="5" class="msg">none of your programs are listening</td></tr>`) + "</tbody>";
}
async function trafficTick() {
  try { S.traffic = await api("/api/traffic"); renderTraffic(); renderOverview(); } catch (e) {}
}
onShow.traffic = renderTraffic;

/* ======================================================== OVERVIEW */
function renderOverview() {
  if (view !== "overview") return;
  const d = S.sys, t = S.traffic, w = S.wifi, b = S.bt, l = S.lan;
  const ks = [];
  if (d) {
    const ramPct = d.mem.total ? d.mem.used / d.mem.total * 100 : 0, root = rootFs(d), romPct = root.used / root.total * 100;
    ks.push(kpi("system", level(d.cpu.total), "Processor", Math.round(d.cpu.total) + "<small>%</small>", `${d.info.cores} threads · load ${d.cpu.load[0].toFixed(2)}`, "system"));
    ks.push(kpi("ram", "violet", "Memory", Math.round(ramPct) + "<small>%</small>", `${fmt(d.mem.used)} of ${fmt(d.mem.total)}`, "system"));
    ks.push(kpi("disk", "teal", "Main drive", Math.round(romPct) + "<small>%</small>", fmt(root.free) + " free", "disk"));
  }
  if (t) {
    const a = t.iface_hist[t.iface_hist.length - 1] || { rx: 0, tx: 0 }, net = t.connections.filter(c => c.kind !== "this computer");
    ks.push(kpi("down", "accent", "Download", rateParts(a.rx), "whole computer", "traffic"));
    ks.push(kpi("up", "teal", "Upload", rateParts(a.tx), "whole computer", "traffic"));
    ks.push(kpi("link", "warn", "Connections", net.length, `${new Set(net.map(c => c.app)).size} programs online`, "traffic"));
    $("ovRx").textContent = rate(a.rx); $("ovTx").textContent = rate(a.tx);
    lineChart("ovChart", [{ data: t.iface_hist.map(x => x.tx), color: "--teal" }, { data: t.iface_hist.map(x => x.rx), color: "--accent" }], { n: 120, label: rate });
    $("ovApps").innerHTML = appRows(t.apps, 5);
  }
  $("ovKpis").innerHTML = ks.join("");

  const tile = (go, icon, l, name, big, sub) => `<div class="card click tile" data-go="${go}" style="--c:${cv(l)};--c-soft:var(--${l}-soft)"><div class="h"><i>${ICON[icon]}</i><b>${name}</b></div><div class="big">${big}</div><div class="d">${sub}</div></div>`;
  const wDev = w && w.devices[0], wNet = w && w.networks.find(n => n.connected);
  const bHeard = b ? b.devices.filter(x => x.rssi != null) : [];
  const lMain = l && (l.connections.find(c => c.plugged_in && c.ipv4) || l.connections[0]);
  const lOk = l && l.health && l.health.internet_ms != null && l.health.dns_ok;
  const temp = d && cpuTemp(d);
  $("ovTiles").innerHTML = [
    tile("wifi", "wifi", "accent", "Wi-Fi", w ? w.networks.length + " networks" : '<span class="spin"></span>', !w ? "scanning…" : wNet ? "connected to " + esc(wNet.ssid) : wDev ? esc(wDev.state) : "no Wi-Fi adapter"),
    tile("bt", "bt", "violet", "Bluetooth", b ? bHeard.length + " devices" : '<span class="spin"></span>', !b ? "listening…" : !b.adapter ? "no adapter" : !b.adapter.powered ? "switched off" : `${b.devices.filter(x => x.paired).length} paired · ${bHeard.filter(x => x.rssi >= -70).length} close by`),
    tile("lan", "lan", lOk ? "good" : "warn", "Network", !l ? '<span class="spin"></span>' : !lMain ? "No cable" : lOk ? "Online" : lMain.plugged_in ? "Limited" : "Unplugged",
      !l ? "checking…" : lMain ? `${esc(lMain.ipv4 || lMain.iface)}${l.health && l.health.internet_ms != null ? " · " + l.health.internet_ms.toFixed(0) + " ms to internet" : ""}` : "wired port not found"),
    tile("system", "temp", temp ? tLevel(temp.c) : "good", "Temperature", temp ? Math.round(temp.c) + "°C" : "n/a", d ? `up ${uptime(d.uptime)}${d.battery ? ` · battery ${d.battery.percent}%` : ""}` : ""),
  ].join("");
}
onShow.overview = renderOverview;

/* ======================================================== DISK */
let dCur = null, dLoaded = false;
async function loadDrives() {
  try {
    const ds = await api("/api/disk/filesystems"); S.drives = ds;
    $("drives").innerHTML = ds.map(d => { const p = Math.round(d.used / d.total * 100), l = level(p) === "accent" ? "teal" : level(p);
      return `<div class="card click drive" data-p="${esc(d.mounts[0])}"><h4><span>${d.mounts.map(esc).join(", ")}</span><span style="color:${cv(l)}">${p}%</span></h4>
        <div class="faint" style="font-size:12px;margin-bottom:10px">${esc(d.device.split("/").pop())} · ${esc(d.fstype)}</div>${bar(p, l)}
        <div class="d"><b style="color:var(--text)">${fmt(d.free)}</b> free of ${fmt(d.total)}</div></div>`; }).join("");
    $("drives").querySelectorAll(".drive").forEach(c => c.onclick = () => openDir(c.dataset.p));
  } catch (e) { $("drives").innerHTML = `<div class="msg err">${esc(e.message)}</div>`; }
}
async function openDir(path, refresh = false) {
  $("dPath").value = path || "";
  $("dList").innerHTML = `<div class="msg"><span class="spin"></span>Measuring ${esc(path || "your home folder")}… big folders take a moment</div>`;
  try {
    const d = await api("/api/disk/dir?" + (path ? "path=" + encodeURIComponent(path) + "&" : "") + (refresh ? "refresh=1" : ""));
    dCur = d; $("dPath").value = d.path;
    const parts = d.path.split("/").filter(Boolean); let acc = "";
    $("dCrumbs").innerHTML = `<a data-p="/">/</a>` + parts.map(p => { acc += "/" + p; return `<span class="sep">›</span><a data-p="${esc(acc)}">${esc(p)}</a>`; }).join("");
    $("dCrumbs").querySelectorAll("a").forEach(a => a.onclick = () => openDir(a.dataset.p));
    const max = d.items.length ? d.items[0].size : 0;
    const icon = { dir: "📁", file: "📄", link: "🔗", mount: "💽" };
    $("dList").innerHTML = `<div class="muted" style="margin-bottom:6px;font-size:13px">Total <b style="color:var(--text)">${fmt(d.total)}</b> · ${d.items.length} items${d.errors ? ` · <span style="color:var(--bad)">${d.errors} unreadable (run with sudo to include)</span>` : ""}</div>
      <div class="tw scroll"><table><tbody>${d.items.map(it => { const p = d.total ? it.size / d.total * 100 : 0, nav = it.type === "dir" || it.type === "mount";
        return `<tr class="${nav ? "click" : ""}" data-p="${esc(it.path)}"><td style="width:28px">${icon[it.type]}</td>
          <td class="wrap ${nav ? "strong" : ""}">${esc(it.name)}${it.type === "mount" ? ' <span class="pill p-mute">other drive</span>' : ""}</td>
          <td class="n">${it.type === "mount" ? "–" : fmt(it.size)}</td><td style="width:30%">${bar(max ? it.size / max * 100 : 0, level(p) === "accent" ? "accent" : level(p))}</td>
          <td class="n muted">${p >= .1 ? p.toFixed(1) + "%" : ""}</td></tr>`; }).join("") || `<tr><td class="msg">empty folder</td></tr>`}</tbody></table></div>`;
    $("dList").querySelectorAll("tr.click").forEach(tr => tr.onclick = () => openDir(tr.dataset.p));
  } catch (e) { $("dList").innerHTML = `<div class="msg err">${esc(e.message)}</div>`; }
}
$("dGo").onclick = () => openDir($("dPath").value.trim());
$("dPath").onkeydown = e => { if (e.key === "Enter") openDir($("dPath").value.trim()); };
$("dUp").onclick = () => dCur && dCur.parent && openDir(dCur.parent);
$("dRe").onclick = () => dCur && openDir(dCur.path, true);
onShow.disk = () => { if (!dLoaded) { dLoaded = true; loadDrives(); openDir(""); } };

/* ======================================================== WIFI */
let wBusy = false, wOpen = new Set(), wLast = 0;
const sigWord = s => s >= 75 ? "Excellent" : s >= 55 ? "Good" : s >= 35 ? "Okay" : "Weak";
async function loadWifi(rescan = false) {
  if (wBusy) return; wBusy = true; $("wScan").disabled = true;
  if (!wLast && view === "wifi") $("wList").innerHTML = `<tr><td class="msg"><span class="spin"></span>Scanning for networks…</td></tr>`;
  try { S.wifi = await api("/api/wifi/scan" + (rescan ? "?rescan=1" : "")); wLast = Date.now(); renderWifi(); renderOverview(); }
  catch (e) { $("wList").innerHTML = `<tr><td class="msg err">${esc(e.message)}</td></tr>`; }
  finally { wBusy = false; $("wScan").disabled = false; }
}
function renderWifi() {
  const d = S.wifi; if (!d) return;
  const dev = d.devices[0], nets = d.networks, off = dev && /unavailable|unmanaged/.test(dev.state);
  $("wStatus").innerHTML = !dev ? banner("bad", "No Wi-Fi adapter found on this computer.")
    : off ? banner("bad", "Wi-Fi is switched off. Turn it on in Settings → Wi-Fi.")
    : dev.state === "connected" ? banner("good", `Connected to <b>&nbsp;${esc(dev.connection)}&nbsp;</b> using adapter ${esc(dev.device)}.`)
    : banner("warn", `Adapter ${esc(dev.device)} is ${esc(dev.state)} — scanning still works.`);
  $("wKpis").innerHTML = [kpi("wifi", "accent", "Networks", nets.length, "visible right now"), kpi("up", "good", "Strong signal", nets.filter(n => n.signal >= 55).length, "good enough to use"),
    kpi("lan", "violet", "Access points", nets.reduce((s, n) => s + n.access_points.length, 0), "radios broadcasting"), kpi("shield", "bad", "Open networks", nets.filter(n => n.security === "Open").length, "no password needed")].join("");
  $("wList").innerHTML = `<thead><tr><th>Network</th><th>Signal</th><th class="n">%</th><th>Bands</th><th>Security</th><th class="n">APs</th></tr></thead><tbody>` +
    nets.map((n, idx) => { const l = sigLevel(n.signal), key = n.ssid || "hidden" + idx;
      const det = n.access_points.map(a => `${a.connected ? "★ " : ""}<span class="mono">${esc(a.bssid)}</span> · ${a.signal}% · ${esc(a.band)} channel ${esc(a.channel)} · ${esc(a.rate)}`).join("<br>");
      return `<tr class="click" data-k="${esc(key)}"><td class="strong">${n.connected ? '<span class="pill p-good">connected</span>' : ""}${n.hidden ? '<span class="faint">hidden network</span>' : esc(n.ssid)}</td>
        <td style="min-width:140px"><div style="display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center">${bar(n.signal, l)}<span style="color:${cv(l)};font-size:12px;font-weight:600;width:62px">${sigWord(n.signal)}</span></div></td><td class="n">${n.signal}</td>
        <td>${n.bands.map(b => `<span class="pill p-mute">${esc(b)}</span>`).join("")}</td>
        <td>${n.security === "Open" ? '<span class="pill p-bad">Open</span>' : '<span class="pill p-good">🔒 ' + esc(n.security) + "</span>"}</td><td class="n">${n.access_points.length}</td></tr>
        <tr class="detail" data-for="${esc(key)}" ${wOpen.has(key) ? "" : "hidden"}><td colspan="6">${det}</td></tr>`; }).join("")
    + (nets.length ? "" : `<tr><td colspan="6" class="msg">no networks visible</td></tr>`) + "</tbody>";
  $("wList").querySelectorAll("tr.click").forEach(tr => tr.onclick = () => {
    const k = tr.dataset.k, det = $("wList").querySelector(`tr.detail[data-for="${CSS.escape(k)}"]`);
    det.hidden = !det.hidden; det.hidden ? wOpen.delete(k) : wOpen.add(k);
  });
}
$("wScan").onclick = () => loadWifi(true);
onShow.wifi = () => { renderWifi(); if (Date.now() - wLast > 15000) loadWifi(false); };

/* ======================================================== BLUETOOTH */
let bFilter = "named", bBusy = false, bLast = 0;
function near(rssi) {
  if (rssi == null) return { w: "not heard", l: "muted", p: 0 };
  const p = Math.max(4, Math.min(100, Math.round((rssi + 100) / 60 * 100)));
  return rssi >= -55 ? { w: "Very close", l: "good", p } : rssi >= -70 ? { w: "Nearby", l: "good", p } : rssi >= -85 ? { w: "Further", l: "warn", p } : { w: "Far away", l: "bad", p };
}
function renderBt() {
  const d = S.bt; if (!d) return;
  const a = d.adapter, devs = d.devices, heard = devs.filter(x => x.rssi != null);
  $("bStatus").innerHTML = !a ? banner("bad", "No Bluetooth adapter found.") : !a.powered ? banner("bad", "Bluetooth is switched off. Turn it on in Settings → Bluetooth.")
    : banner("good", `Bluetooth is on · this computer appears as “${esc(a.name)}”.`);
  $("bKpis").innerHTML = [kpi("bt", "violet", "Devices heard", heard.length, "in the last scan"), kpi("ear", "good", "Close to you", heard.filter(x => x.rssi >= -70).length, "within a few metres"),
    kpi("overview", "accent", "With a name", heard.filter(x => x.name).length, ""), kpi("link", "teal", "Paired", devs.filter(x => x.paired).length, "remembered by this PC")].join("");
  let list = devs;
  if (bFilter === "named") list = devs.filter(x => x.paired || (x.name && x.rssi != null));
  if (bFilter === "mine") list = devs.filter(x => x.paired);
  $("bList").innerHTML = `<thead><tr><th></th><th>Name</th><th>Type</th><th>Maker</th><th>Distance</th><th class="n">dBm</th><th>Status</th></tr></thead><tbody>` +
    (list.map(x => { const n = near(x.rssi);
      return `<tr><td style="font-size:18px">${x.emoji}</td><td class="strong">${x.name ? esc(x.name) : '<span class="faint">unnamed</span>'}</td><td>${esc(x.kind)}</td><td class="muted">${esc(x.maker || "")}</td>
        <td style="min-width:150px"><div style="display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center">${bar(n.p, n.l)}<span style="color:${cv(n.l)};font-size:12px;font-weight:600;width:70px">${n.w}</span></div></td><td class="n">${x.rssi ?? ""}</td>
        <td>${x.connected ? '<span class="pill p-good">connected</span>' : ""}${x.paired ? '<span class="pill">paired</span>' : ""}${x.battery != null ? `<span class="pill p-teal">battery ${x.battery}%</span>` : ""}</td></tr>`; }).join("")
     || `<tr><td colspan="7" class="msg">Nothing here. Try “Everything” or scan again.</td></tr>`) + "</tbody>";
}
async function loadBt(scan) {
  if (bBusy) return; bBusy = true; $("bScan").disabled = true;
  let t; if (scan) { const s = Date.now(); t = setInterval(() => $("bBar").style.width = Math.min(100, (Date.now() - s) / 90) + "%", 100); $("bScan").innerHTML = '<span class="spin"></span>Listening…'; }
  try { S.bt = await api("/api/bt/devices" + (scan ? "?scan=1" : "")); if (scan) bLast = Date.now(); renderBt(); renderOverview(); }
  catch (e) { $("bList").innerHTML = `<tr><td class="msg err">${esc(e.message)}</td></tr>`; }
  finally { clearInterval(t); $("bBar").style.width = "0"; bBusy = false; $("bScan").disabled = false; $("bScan").textContent = "Scan"; }
}
$("bFilter").onclick = e => { const b = e.target.closest("button"); if (!b) return; bFilter = b.dataset.f; $("bFilter").querySelectorAll("button").forEach(x => x.classList.toggle("on", x === b)); renderBt(); };
$("bScan").onclick = () => loadBt(true);
onShow.bt = async () => { renderBt(); if (!S.bt) await loadBt(false); if (Date.now() - bLast > 30000) loadBt(true); };

/* ======================================================== NETWORK */
function lat(ms, home) { if (ms == null) return ["no answer", "bad"]; const ok = home ? 5 : 50, meh = home ? 20 : 120; return [ms.toFixed(1) + " ms", ms <= ok ? "good" : ms <= meh ? "warn" : "bad"]; }
async function loadLan() {
  $("lCheck").disabled = true;
  try { S.lan = await api("/api/lan/connection"); renderLan(); renderOverview(); }
  catch (e) { $("lStatus").innerHTML = banner("bad", esc(e.message)); }
  finally { $("lCheck").disabled = false; }
}
function renderLan() {
  const d = S.lan; if (!d) return;
  const cs = d.connections, h = d.health;
  if (!cs.length) { $("lStatus").innerHTML = banner("warn", "No wired network port found on this computer. Wi-Fi traffic still shows in Traffic."); $("lKv").innerHTML = ""; return; }
  const c = cs.find(x => x.plugged_in && x.ipv4) || cs.find(x => x.plugged_in) || cs[0];
  let st;
  if (!c.plugged_in) st = ["bad", `Cable unplugged on ${esc(c.iface)}.`];
  else if (!c.ipv4) st = ["warn", "Cable connected — waiting for an IP address."];
  else if (h && h.internet_ms != null && h.dns_ok) st = ["good", `Online via ${esc(c.connection || c.iface)} — everything is working.`];
  else if (h && h.router_ms != null) st = ["bad", "Router answers, but the internet does not."];
  else st = ["bad", "Cable connected, but the router is not answering."];
  $("lStatus").innerHTML = banner(...st);
  const rt = h ? (h.router_ms == null && h.internet_ms != null ? ["ignores ping (ok)", "warn"] : lat(h.router_ms, true)) : ["–", "muted"];
  const it = h ? lat(h.internet_ms, false) : ["–", "muted"];
  $("lKv").innerHTML = [
    ["Link speed", c.speed_mbps ? c.speed_mbps + " Mbit/s" : "–", c.speed_mbps >= 1000 ? "gigabit · fast" : c.speed_mbps ? "check the cable if you expected gigabit" : ""],
    ["Router ping", `<span style="color:${cv(rt[1])}">${rt[0]}</span>`, "how fast your router answers"],
    ["Internet ping", `<span style="color:${cv(it[1])}">${it[0]}</span>`, "time to reach 1.1.1.1"],
    ["DNS", h ? (h.dns_ok ? '<span style="color:var(--good)">working</span>' : '<span style="color:var(--bad)">failing</span>') : "–", "turns names into addresses"],
    ["Your IP", esc(c.ipv4 || "–"), c.dhcp ? "given by the router (DHCP)" : "set manually"],
    ["Gateway", esc(c.gateway || "–"), "your router"],
    ["DNS server", esc(c.dns.join(", ") || "–"), ""],
    ["MAC address", `<span class="mono">${esc(c.mac || "–")}</span>`, "port " + esc(c.iface)],
  ].map(([k, v, e]) => `<div><div class="k">${k}</div><div class="v">${v}</div><div class="e">${e}</div></div>`).join("");
}
async function scanLan() {
  $("lScan").disabled = true; $("lScan").innerHTML = '<span class="spin"></span>Searching…';
  const s = Date.now(), t = setInterval(() => $("lBar").style.width = Math.min(95, (Date.now() - s) / 80) + "%", 100);
  try {
    const d = await api("/api/lan/devices"); S.lanDevs = d;
    $("lNet").textContent = d.network ? `${d.network} · ${d.devices.length} devices answered` : "";
    $("lDevs").innerHTML = !d.network ? `<div class="msg">No wired network connected.</div>` : `<div class="tw"><table><thead><tr><th></th><th>Name</th><th>IP address</th><th>Type</th><th>Maker</th><th class="n">Ping</th><th>MAC</th><th></th></tr></thead><tbody>` +
      d.devices.map(x => `<tr><td style="font-size:18px">${x.emoji}</td>
        <td class="strong">${x.name ? esc(x.name) : '<span class="faint">unknown</span>'} ${x.is_self ? '<span class="pill p-good">you</span>' : ""}${x.is_gateway ? '<span class="pill p-warn">router</span>' : ""}</td>
        <td class="mono">${esc(x.ip)}</td><td>${esc(x.kind)}</td><td class="muted">${esc(x.vendor || (x.private_mac ? "private address" : ""))}</td>
        <td class="n">${x.is_self ? "" : x.ping_ms != null ? x.ping_ms.toFixed(1) + " ms" : '<span class="faint">silent</span>'}</td>
        <td class="mono faint">${esc(x.mac || "")}</td><td><button class="btn sm" data-ip="${esc(x.ip)}">Check ports</button></td></tr>`).join("") + `</tbody></table></div>`;
    $("lDevs").querySelectorAll("[data-ip]").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { $("pHost").value = b.dataset.ip; show("ports"); $("pHost").focus(); });
  } catch (e) { $("lDevs").innerHTML = `<div class="msg err">${esc(e.message)}</div>`; }
  finally { clearInterval(t); $("lBar").style.width = "0"; $("lScan").disabled = false; $("lScan").textContent = "Find devices"; }
}
$("lCheck").onclick = loadLan; $("lScan").onclick = scanLan;
onShow.lan = () => { if (S.lan) renderLan(); else loadLan(); };

/* ======================================================== PORTS */
let preset = "web", jobId = null, pTimer = null;
const LV = { web: ["good", "Normal for a web server"], ok: ["good", "Fine if you use it"], check: ["warn", "Check you meant to open this"], risky: ["bad", "Usually shouldn't be reachable"] };
$("pPresets").onclick = e => {
  const c = e.target.closest(".chip"); if (!c) return;
  $("pPresets").querySelectorAll(".chip").forEach(x => x.classList.toggle("on", x === c));
  preset = c.dataset.p; $("pCustom").hidden = preset !== "custom"; if (preset === "custom") $("pCustom").focus();
};
function found(f) {
  if (!f) return "";
  if (f.kind === "http") return `Website: ${esc(f.status)}${f.server ? " · " + esc(f.server) : ""}`;
  if (f.kind === "https") return `🔐 Secure website (${esc(f.tls)}): ${esc(f.status)}${f.server ? " · " + esc(f.server) : ""}`;
  if (f.kind === "tls") return `🔐 Encrypted service (${esc(f.tls)})`;
  if (f.kind === "banner") return `Says: “${esc(f.text)}”`;
  return "";
}
function renderPorts(j) {
  S.ports = j;
  const pct = j.total ? j.done / j.total * 100 : 0, risky = j.open.filter(p => p.level === "risky").length;
  const verdict = !j.finished ? null : !j.open.length ? ["accent", "No open ports among the ones checked."]
    : risky ? ["bad", `${risky} open port${risky > 1 ? "s" : ""} that usually shouldn't be reachable — review below.`] : ["good", "Nothing dangerous exposed — looks clean."];
  $("pResult").innerHTML = `<div class="card">
    <div class="card-h"><h3>${j.finished ? "Result" : '<span class="spin"></span>Checking'} · ${esc(j.host)}${j.host !== j.ip ? ` <span class="faint">(${esc(j.ip)})</span>` : ""}</h3>
      <span class="sub">${j.done.toLocaleString()} of ${j.total.toLocaleString()} ports · ${j.elapsed}s</span>
      <div class="r">${j.finished ? "" : '<button class="btn sm danger" id="pStop">Stop</button>'}</div></div>
    <div class="progress"><span style="width:${pct}%"></span></div>
    ${verdict ? banner(...verdict) : ""}
    <div class="kpis" style="margin-bottom:0">${kpi("ports", "good", "Open", j.open.length, "")}${kpi("shield", "accent", "Closed", j.closed.toLocaleString(), "")}${kpi("shield", "violet", "Blocked by firewall", j.filtered.toLocaleString(), "")}${kpi("ear", "bad", "Worth a look", risky, "")}</div>
    <div class="portcards">${j.open.map(p => { const [l, word] = LV[p.level] || LV.check;
      return `<div class="portcard" style="--c:${cv(l)}"><b>${p.port} · ${esc(p.name)}</b><div class="w">${word}</div><div class="d">${esc(p.desc)}</div>${found(p.found) ? `<div class="d" style="color:var(--text)">${found(p.found)}</div>` : ""}</div>`; }).join("")}</div></div>`;
  if ($("pStop")) $("pStop").onclick = () => fetch("/api/ports/cancel?id=" + jobId);
}
async function startPorts() {
  const host = $("pHost").value.trim(), spec = preset === "custom" ? $("pCustom").value.trim() : preset;
  if (!host) { $("pResult").innerHTML = banner("warn", "Enter an address to check first."); return; }
  clearInterval(pTimer); $("pStart").disabled = true;
  try {
    jobId = (await api(`/api/ports/start?host=${encodeURIComponent(host)}&ports=${encodeURIComponent(spec)}`)).id;
    const poll = async () => {
      try { const j = await api("/api/ports/status?id=" + jobId); renderPorts(j); if (j.finished) { clearInterval(pTimer); $("pStart").disabled = false; } }
      catch (e) { clearInterval(pTimer); $("pResult").innerHTML = banner("bad", esc(e.message)); $("pStart").disabled = false; }
    };
    pTimer = setInterval(poll, 700); poll();
  } catch (e) { $("pResult").innerHTML = banner("bad", esc(e.message)); $("pStart").disabled = false; }
}
$("pStart").onclick = startPorts;
$("pHost").onkeydown = $("pCustom").onkeydown = e => { if (e.key === "Enter") startPorts(); };

/* ======================================================== EXPORT (CSV / PDF) */
let toastT;
function toast(msg) { const t = $("toast"); t.textContent = msg; t.classList.add("show"); clearTimeout(toastT); toastT = setTimeout(() => t.classList.remove("show"), 3200); }
function stamp() { const d = new Date(), p = n => String(n).padStart(2, "0"); return `${d.getFullYear()}-${p(d.getMonth() + 1)}-${p(d.getDate())}_${p(d.getHours())}${p(d.getMinutes())}`; }
function csvCell(v) {
  if (v == null) return "";
  if (typeof v === "number") return Number.isInteger(v) ? String(v) : v.toFixed(2);
  if (typeof v === "boolean") return v ? "yes" : "no";
  let s = String(v);
  if (/^[=+@\t\r]/.test(s) || (/^-/.test(s) && isNaN(s))) s = "'" + s;   // stop spreadsheets treating text as a formula
  return /[",\n\r]/.test(s) ? '"' + s.replace(/"/g, '""') + '"' : s;
}
const kbit = bps => +((bps || 0) * 8 / 1000).toFixed(1);
const NO_DATA = msg => { throw new Error(msg); };

/* each page returns a list of sections: { title, head: [...], rows: [[...], ...] } */
const EXPORTS = {
  overview() {
    const d = S.sys, t = S.traffic, rows = [];
    if (d) {
      const root = rootFs(d), temp = cpuTemp(d);
      rows.push(["Computer", d.info.hostname], ["System", d.info.os], ["Uptime", uptime(d.uptime)], ["Processor load (%)", +d.cpu.total.toFixed(1)],
        ["Memory used (%)", +(d.mem.used / d.mem.total * 100).toFixed(1)], ["Main drive used (%)", +(root.used / root.total * 100).toFixed(1)],
        ["Main drive free (bytes)", root.free], ["CPU temperature (C)", temp ? temp.c : ""], ["Battery (%)", d.battery ? d.battery.percent : ""]);
    }
    if (t) {
      const a = t.iface_hist[t.iface_hist.length - 1] || { rx: 0, tx: 0 }, net = t.connections.filter(c => c.kind !== "this computer");
      rows.push(["Download now (kbit/s)", kbit(a.rx)], ["Upload now (kbit/s)", kbit(a.tx)], ["Open connections", net.length],
        ["Data received this session (bytes)", t.session.recv], ["Data sent this session (bytes)", t.session.sent]);
    }
    if (S.wifi) rows.push(["Wi-Fi networks visible", S.wifi.networks.length]);
    if (S.bt) rows.push(["Bluetooth devices heard", S.bt.devices.filter(x => x.rssi != null).length]);
    if (S.lan && S.lan.health) rows.push(["Internet ping (ms)", S.lan.health.internet_ms ?? "no answer"], ["DNS working", S.lan.health.dns_ok]);
    if (!rows.length) NO_DATA("Still loading — try again in a second.");
    return [{ title: "Overview", head: ["Item", "Value"], rows }];
  },
  system() {
    const d = S.sys; if (!d) NO_DATA("Still loading — try again in a second.");
    const procs = [...d.procs.by_cpu, ...d.procs.by_mem.filter(p => !d.procs.by_cpu.some(q => q.pid === p.pid))];
    return [
      { title: "Summary", head: ["Item", "Value"], rows: [["Computer", d.info.hostname], ["System", d.info.os], ["Kernel", d.info.kernel], ["Processor", d.info.cpu_model],
        ["Threads", d.info.cores], ["Uptime", uptime(d.uptime)], ["Processor load (%)", d.cpu.total], ["Load 1/5/15 min", d.cpu.load.map(x => x.toFixed(2)).join(" / ")],
        ["Memory total (bytes)", d.mem.total], ["Memory used (bytes)", d.mem.used], ["Memory available (bytes)", d.mem.available], ["Cache (bytes)", d.mem.cache],
        ["Swap total (bytes)", d.mem.swap_total], ["Swap used (bytes)", d.mem.swap_used], ["Processes", d.procs.count], ["Battery (%)", d.battery ? d.battery.percent : ""]] },
      { title: "Cores", head: ["Core", "Load (%)", "Speed (MHz)"], rows: d.cpu.cores.map((p, i) => [i, p, d.cpu.freqs[i] || ""]) },
      { title: "Storage", head: ["Mounted at", "Device", "Type", "Total (bytes)", "Used (bytes)", "Free (bytes)", "Used (%)"],
        rows: d.storage.map(s => [s.mounts.join(" "), s.device, s.fstype, s.total, s.used, s.free, s.used / s.total * 100]) },
      { title: "Disk activity", head: ["Disk", "Type", "Size (bytes)", "Reading (bytes/s)", "Writing (bytes/s)"], rows: d.io.map(x => [x.disk, x.ssd ? "SSD" : "HDD", x.size, x.read, x.write]) },
      { title: "Top processes", head: ["Program", "PID", "User", "State", "CPU (%)", "Memory (%)", "RAM (bytes)"],
        rows: procs.map(p => [p.name, p.pid, p.user, p.state, p.cpu, p.mem_pct, p.mem]) },
      { title: "Temperatures", head: ["Sensor", "Label", "Celsius"], rows: d.temps.map(t => [t.sensor, t.label, t.c]) },
    ];
  },
  disk() {
    const out = [];
    if (S.drives) out.push({ title: "Drives", head: ["Mounted at", "Device", "Type", "Total (bytes)", "Used (bytes)", "Free (bytes)", "Used (%)"],
      rows: S.drives.map(d => [d.mounts.join(" "), d.device, d.fstype, d.total, d.used, d.free, d.used / d.total * 100]) });
    if (dCur) out.push({ title: "Folder " + dCur.path, head: ["Name", "Type", "Size (bytes)", "Share of folder (%)", "Full path"],
      rows: dCur.items.map(it => [it.name, it.type, it.type === "mount" ? "" : it.size, dCur.total ? it.size / dCur.total * 100 : 0, it.path]) });
    if (!out.length) NO_DATA("Open the Disk page first so there is something to export.");
    return out;
  },
  wifi() {
    const d = S.wifi; if (!d) NO_DATA("No Wi-Fi scan yet — press “Scan again” first.");
    return [
      { title: "Networks", head: ["Network", "Connected", "Signal (%)", "Quality", "Bands", "Security", "Access points"],
        rows: d.networks.map(n => [n.hidden ? "(hidden)" : n.ssid, n.connected, n.signal, sigWord(n.signal), n.bands.join(" + "), n.security, n.access_points.length]) },
      { title: "Access points", head: ["Network", "BSSID", "Signal (%)", "Band", "Channel", "Rate", "Connected"],
        rows: d.networks.flatMap(n => n.access_points.map(a => [n.hidden ? "(hidden)" : n.ssid, a.bssid, a.signal, a.band, a.channel, a.rate, a.connected])) },
    ];
  },
  bt() {
    const d = S.bt; if (!d) NO_DATA("No Bluetooth scan yet — press “Scan” first.");
    return [{ title: "Bluetooth devices", head: ["Name", "Address", "Type", "Maker", "Signal (dBm)", "Distance", "Paired", "Connected", "Battery (%)"],
      rows: d.devices.map(x => [x.name || "(unnamed)", x.address, x.kind, x.maker || "", x.rssi ?? "", near(x.rssi).w, x.paired, x.connected, x.battery ?? ""]) }];
  },
  lan() {
    const out = [], d = S.lan;
    if (d && d.connections.length) {
      const c = d.connections.find(x => x.plugged_in && x.ipv4) || d.connections[0], h = d.health || {};
      out.push({ title: "Wired connection", head: ["Item", "Value"], rows: [["Port", c.iface], ["Cable plugged in", c.plugged_in], ["Connection", c.connection || ""],
        ["Link speed (Mbit/s)", c.speed_mbps || ""], ["IP address", c.ipv4 || ""], ["Gateway", c.gateway || ""], ["DNS servers", c.dns.join(" ")], ["MAC address", c.mac || ""],
        ["Router ping (ms)", h.router_ms ?? "no answer"], ["Internet ping (ms)", h.internet_ms ?? "no answer"], ["DNS working", h.dns_ok]] });
    }
    if (S.lanDevs) out.push({ title: "Devices on network " + (S.lanDevs.network || ""), head: ["Name", "IP address", "Type", "Maker", "Ping (ms)", "MAC address", "Note"],
      rows: S.lanDevs.devices.map(x => [x.name || "", x.ip, x.kind, x.vendor || "", x.ping_ms ?? "", x.mac || "", x.is_self ? "this computer" : x.is_gateway ? "router" : ""]) });
    if (!out.length) NO_DATA("Nothing to export yet — press “Check again” or “Find devices”.");
    return out;
  },
  ports() {
    const j = S.ports; if (!j) NO_DATA("Run a port check first.");
    return [
      { title: "Port check", head: ["Item", "Value"], rows: [["Target", j.host], ["IP address", j.ip], ["Ports checked", j.done], ["Finished", j.finished],
        ["Open", j.open.length], ["Closed", j.closed], ["Blocked by firewall", j.filtered], ["Seconds", j.elapsed]] },
      { title: "Open ports", head: ["Port", "Service", "Verdict", "Description", "Found"],
        rows: j.open.map(p => [p.port, p.name, (LV[p.level] || LV.check)[1], p.desc, found(p.found).replace(/<[^>]+>/g, "")]) },
    ];
  },
  traffic() {
    const d = S.traffic; if (!d) NO_DATA("Still loading — try again in a second.");
    return [
      { title: "Session", head: ["Item", "Value"], rows: [["User", d.user], ["Watching since", new Date(d.started * 1000).toLocaleString()],
        ["Data received (bytes)", d.session.recv], ["Data sent (bytes)", d.session.sent], ["Connections of other users / system", d.other_connections]] },
      { title: "Connections", head: ["Direction", "Program", "PID", "Remote name", "Remote address", "Remote port", "Local port", "Protocol", "Service", "Where", "State",
        "Received (bytes)", "Sent (bytes)", "Download (kbit/s)", "Upload (kbit/s)"],
        rows: d.connections.map(c => [c.direction === "in" ? "incoming" : "outgoing", c.app, c.pid ?? "", c.name, c.remote, c.rport, c.lport, c.proto.toUpperCase(), c.service, c.kind,
          c.state, c.proto === "tcp" ? c.recv : "", c.proto === "tcp" ? c.sent : "", kbit(c.in_rate), kbit(c.out_rate)]) },
      { title: "Programs", head: ["Program", "Open connections", "Received this session (bytes)", "Sent this session (bytes)", "Download now (kbit/s)", "Upload now (kbit/s)", "Talking to"],
        rows: d.apps.map(a => [a.app, a.connections, a.recv, a.sent, kbit(a.in_rate), kbit(a.out_rate), a.remotes.join(" ")]) },
      { title: "Listening ports", head: ["Program", "PID", "Port", "Protocol", "Service", "Reachable from", "Address"],
        rows: d.listening.map(l => [l.app || "unknown", l.pid ?? "", l.port, l.proto.toUpperCase(), l.service, l.open_to, l.ip]) },
      { title: "Speed history (whole computer)", head: ["Time", "Download (kbit/s)", "Upload (kbit/s)"],
        rows: d.iface_hist.map(x => [new Date(x.t * 1000).toLocaleTimeString(), kbit(x.rx), kbit(x.tx)]) },
    ];
  },
};
function exportCsv() {
  let sections;
  try { sections = EXPORTS[view](); } catch (e) { toast(e.message); return; }
  const lines = [`Collective Scanner - ${VIEWS.find(v => v[0] === view)[1]}`, `Exported,${csvCell(new Date().toLocaleString())}`, ""];
  for (const s of sections) {
    lines.push(csvCell(s.title), s.head.map(csvCell).join(","), ...s.rows.map(r => r.map(csvCell).join(",")), "");
  }
  const blob = new Blob(["﻿" + lines.join("\r\n")], { type: "text/csv;charset=utf-8" });
  const a = document.createElement("a");
  a.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob); a.download = `collective-scanner_${view}_${stamp()}.csv`;
  document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); a.remove();
  setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href), 2000);
  toast("CSV saved to your Downloads folder");
}
function exportPdf() {
  const before = document.documentElement.dataset.theme, title = document.title;
  $("printMeta").innerHTML = `${esc(VIEWS.find(v => v[0] === view)[1])} report<br>${esc(S.sys ? S.sys.info.hostname : "")} · ${esc(new Date().toLocaleString())}`;
  document.title = `collective-scanner_${view}_${stamp()}`;           // becomes the suggested PDF file name
  if (before !== "light") { document.documentElement.dataset.theme = "light"; }
  requestAnimationFrame(() => {
    redrawCharts();
    setTimeout(() => {
      window.print();
      document.title = title;
      if (before !== "light") setTheme(before, false);
    }, 120);
  });
  toast("Choose “Save as PDF” as the printer");
}
$("expBtn").onclick = e => { e.stopPropagation(); $("expMenu").hidden = !$("expMenu").hidden; };
$("expMenu").onclick = e => {
  const b = e.target.closest("button"); if (!b) return;
  $("expMenu").hidden = true;
  b.dataset.x === "csv" ? exportCsv() : exportPdf();
};
document.addEventListener("click", e => { if (!e.target.closest(".export")) $("expMenu").hidden = true; });
addEventListener("keydown", e => { if (e.key === "Escape") $("expMenu").hidden = true; });

/* ======================================================== start */
setTheme(document.documentElement.dataset.theme, false);
let start = "overview";
try { start = localStorage.getItem("cs-view") || "overview"; } catch (e) {}
if (VIEWS.some(v => v[0] === location.hash.slice(1))) start = location.hash.slice(1);
show(start);
sysTick(); setInterval(sysTick, 1000);
trafficTick(); setInterval(trafficTick, 2000);
loadWifi(false); loadBt(false); loadLan();
setInterval(() => { if (view === "wifi" || view === "overview") loadWifi(false); }, 20000);
setInterval(() => { if (view === "bt") loadBt(true); }, 30000);
setInterval(() => { if (view === "lan" || view === "overview") loadLan(); }, 60000);
</script>
</body>
</html>
"""


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="collective-scanner",
                                 description="Collective Scanner - all scanners plus live traffic in one dashboard")
    ap.add_argument("--host", default="127.0.0.1", help="address to listen on (default: this computer only)")
    ap.add_argument("--port", type=int, default=8780)
    ap.add_argument("--open", action="store_true", help="open the dashboard in your web browser")
    ap.add_argument("--version", action="version", version=f"%(prog)s {__version__}")
    args = ap.parse_args()
    url = f"http://{args.host}:{args.port}"
    try:
        server = ThreadingHTTPServer((args.host, args.port), Handler)
    except OSError as e:
        if e.errno == 98:
            if args.open:  # already running (e.g. started from the app menu before) - just show it
                webbrowser.open(url)
                return
            print(f"Port {args.port} is already in use - Collective Scanner is probably already running.\n"
                  f"Open {url} in your browser, or stop the other copy with:\n"
                  f"    pkill -f collective.py\n"
                  f"or start this one on another port:  --port {args.port + 1}")
            sys.exit(1)
        raise
    threading.Thread(target=sysmon.sampler, daemon=True).start()
    traffic.start()
    print(f"Collective Scanner {__version__} is running at {url}  (Ctrl+C to stop)", flush=True)
    if args.open:
        threading.Timer(0.8, webbrowser.open, [url]).start()
    try:
        server.serve_forever()
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        print("\nCollective Scanner stopped.")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

#!/usr/bin/env python3
# SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-or-later
# Copyright (C) 2026 Denis
"""Disk Usage Viewer - a tiny local web app (Python standard library only).

Run:   python3 diskusage.py            then open http://127.0.0.1:8765
Opts:  --port 9000   --host 127.0.0.1

Shows:
  * every mounted disk/partition with total, used and free space
  * a folder explorer: click into folders to see what takes up the space
"""

import argparse
import json
import os
import stat
import threading
from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer
from urllib.parse import parse_qs, urlparse

# Filesystem types that are not real disks and only add noise.
PSEUDO_FS = {
    "proc", "sysfs", "devtmpfs", "devpts", "tmpfs", "securityfs", "cgroup",
    "cgroup2", "pstore", "bpf", "debugfs", "tracefs", "configfs", "fusectl",
    "mqueue", "hugetlbfs", "autofs", "efivarfs", "selinuxfs", "binfmt_misc",
    "rpc_pipefs", "nsfs", "ramfs", "squashfs", "overlay", "fuse.portal",
    "fuse.gvfsd-fuse",
}

_cache = {}
_cache_lock = threading.Lock()


def list_filesystems():
    """Read /proc/mounts and return usage for each real filesystem."""
    seen = {}
    with open("/proc/mounts") as f:
        for line in f:
            device, mount, fstype, *_ = line.split()
            mount = mount.replace("\\040", " ")
            if fstype in PSEUDO_FS or not device.startswith("/"):
                continue
            try:
                st = os.statvfs(mount)
            except OSError:
                continue
            total = st.f_blocks * st.f_frsize
            if total == 0:
                continue
            free = st.f_bavail * st.f_frsize
            used = (st.f_blocks - st.f_bfree) * st.f_frsize
            # btrfs subvolumes (e.g. / and /home on Fedora) share one disk:
            # group them so the same space isn't shown twice.
            key = (device, total)
            if key in seen:
                seen[key]["mounts"].append(mount)
                continue
            seen[key] = {
                "device": device,
                "fstype": fstype,
                "mounts": [mount],
                "total": total,
                "used": used,
                "free": free,
            }
    return sorted(seen.values(), key=lambda x: x["mounts"][0])


def tree_size(path, dev, seen_inodes):
    """Disk space used by `path` (like `du`), staying on one filesystem."""
    total = 0
    errors = 0
    stack = [path]
    while stack:
        current = stack.pop()
        try:
            it = os.scandir(current)
        except OSError:
            errors += 1
            continue
        with it:
            for entry in it:
                try:
                    st = entry.stat(follow_symlinks=False)
                except OSError:
                    errors += 1
                    continue
                if st.st_dev != dev:
                    continue  # another mounted filesystem, skip
                if st.st_nlink > 1:
                    key = (st.st_dev, st.st_ino)
                    if key in seen_inodes:
                        continue
                    seen_inodes.add(key)
                total += st.st_blocks * 512
                if stat.S_ISDIR(st.st_mode):
                    stack.append(entry.path)
    return total, errors


def scan_dir(path, refresh=False):
    """List the direct children of `path` with their sizes."""
    path = os.path.realpath(path)
    with _cache_lock:
        if not refresh and path in _cache:
            return _cache[path]

    root_st = os.stat(path)
    if not stat.S_ISDIR(root_st.st_mode):
        raise NotADirectoryError(path)
    dev = root_st.st_dev
    seen = set()
    items = []
    files_total = 0
    files_count = 0
    errors = 0

    with os.scandir(path) as it:
        entries = list(it)

    for entry in entries:
        try:
            st = entry.stat(follow_symlinks=False)
        except OSError:
            errors += 1
            continue
        if stat.S_ISDIR(st.st_mode):
            if st.st_dev != dev:
                items.append({"name": entry.name, "path": entry.path,
                              "type": "mount", "size": 0})
                continue
            size, err = tree_size(entry.path, dev, seen)
            size += st.st_blocks * 512
            errors += err
            items.append({"name": entry.name, "path": entry.path,
                          "type": "dir", "size": size})
        else:
            size = st.st_blocks * 512
            files_count += 1
            files_total += size
            items.append({"name": entry.name, "path": entry.path,
                          "type": "link" if stat.S_ISLNK(st.st_mode) else "file",
                          "size": size})

    items.sort(key=lambda x: x["size"], reverse=True)
    result = {
        "path": path,
        "parent": os.path.dirname(path) if path != "/" else None,
        "total": sum(i["size"] for i in items),
        "items": items,
        "errors": errors,
    }
    with _cache_lock:
        _cache[path] = result
    return result


class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
    def _send(self, code, body, ctype):
        data = body.encode() if isinstance(body, str) else body
        self.send_response(code)
        self.send_header("Content-Type", ctype)
        self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(data)))
        self.end_headers()
        self.wfile.write(data)

    def _json(self, obj, code=200):
        self._send(code, json.dumps(obj), "application/json")

    def do_GET(self):
        url = urlparse(self.path)
        q = parse_qs(url.query)
        try:
            if url.path == "/":
                self._send(200, PAGE, "text/html; charset=utf-8")
            elif url.path == "/api/filesystems":
                self._json(list_filesystems())
            elif url.path == "/api/dir":
                path = q.get("path", [os.path.expanduser("~")])[0]
                refresh = q.get("refresh", ["0"])[0] == "1"
                self._json(scan_dir(path, refresh))
            else:
                self._json({"error": "not found"}, 404)
        except PermissionError:
            self._json({"error": "Permission denied (try running with sudo to see everything)"}, 403)
        except (FileNotFoundError, NotADirectoryError) as e:
            self._json({"error": f"Not a folder: {e}"}, 400)
        except Exception as e:  # keep the server alive on unexpected errors
            self._json({"error": str(e)}, 500)

    def log_message(self, fmt, *args):
        pass  # keep the terminal quiet


PAGE = r"""<!doctype html>

           background: var(--card); color: var(--text); font: inherit; cursor: pointer; }

  <p class="sub">Your drives and what is taking up space. Click a drive or folder to look inside.</p>

  <h2>Drives</h2>
  <div class="disks" id="disks"><div class="loading">Loading…</div></div>

  <h2>Folder explorer</h2>

<script>
const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);
let current = null;

function fmt(bytes) {
  const u = ["B", "KB", "MB", "GB", "TB"];
  let i = 0;
  while (bytes >= 1024 && i < u.length - 1) { bytes /= 1024; i++; }
  return (i === 0 ? bytes : bytes.toFixed(bytes < 10 ? 1 : 0)) + " " + u[i];
}
function color(pct) {
  return pct >= 90 ? "var(--bad)" : pct >= 75 ? "var(--warn)" : "var(--ok)";
}
function esc(s) {
  return s.replace(/[&<>"]/g, c => ({"&":"&amp;","<":"&lt;",">":"&gt;",'"':"&quot;"}[c]));
}

async function loadDisks() {
  const res = await fetch("/api/filesystems");
  const disks = await res.json();
  $("disks").innerHTML = disks.map(d => {
    const pct = d.total ? Math.round(d.used / d.total * 100) : 0;
    return `<div class="card disk" data-path="${esc(d.mounts[0])}">
      <div class="mount">${d.mounts.map(esc).join(", ")}</div>
      <div class="dev">${esc(d.device)} · ${esc(d.fstype)}</div>
      <div class="pct" style="color:${color(pct)}">${pct}%</div>
      <div class="bar"><span style="width:${pct}%;background:${color(pct)}"></span></div>
      <div class="nums"><span>${fmt(d.used)} used</span><span>${fmt(d.free)} free of ${fmt(d.total)}</span></div>
    </div>`;
  }).join("");
  document.querySelectorAll(".disk").forEach(el =>
    el.addEventListener("click", () => openDir(el.dataset.path)));
}

function renderCrumbs(path) {
  const parts = path.split("/").filter(Boolean);
  let acc = "";
  const links = [`<a data-p="/">/</a>`];
  parts.forEach((p, i) => {
    acc += "/" + p;
    links.push(`<a data-p="${esc(acc)}">${esc(p)}</a>`);
  });
  $("crumbs").innerHTML = links.join(" <span style='color:var(--muted)'>›</span> ");
  $("crumbs").querySelectorAll("a").forEach(a =>
    a.addEventListener("click", () => openDir(a.dataset.p)));
}

async function openDir(path, refresh = false) {
  $("pathInput").value = path || "";
  $("listing").innerHTML = `<div class="loading">Scanning ${esc(path || "home folder")}… (big folders can take a while)</div>`;
  const url = "/api/dir?" + (path ? "path=" + encodeURIComponent(path) + "&" : "") + (refresh ? "refresh=1" : "");
  const res = await fetch(url);
  const data = await res.json();
  if (!res.ok) {
    $("listing").innerHTML = `<div class="error">${esc(data.error)}</div>`;
    return;
  }
  current = data;
  $("pathInput").value = data.path;
  renderCrumbs(data.path);
  const max = data.items.length ? data.items[0].size : 0;
  const icons = { dir: "📁", file: "📄", link: "🔗", mount: "💽" };
  const rows = data.items.map(it => {
    const pct = data.total ? it.size / data.total * 100 : 0;
    const w = max ? it.size / max * 100 : 0;
    const click = it.type === "dir" || it.type === "mount";
    const label = it.type === "mount" ? " <span style='color:var(--muted)'>(other drive)</span>" : "";
    return `<tr class="${click ? "clickable" : ""}" data-p="${esc(it.path)}">
      <td class="name"><span class="icon">${icons[it.type]}</span>${esc(it.name)}${label}</td>
      <td class="size">${it.type === "mount" ? "–" : fmt(it.size)}</td>
      <td class="share"><div class="bar"><span style="width:${w}%;background:var(--accent)"></span></div></td>
      <td class="pc">${pct >= 0.1 ? pct.toFixed(1) + "%" : ""}</td>
    </tr>`;
  }).join("");
  $("listing").innerHTML = `
    <div class="note" style="margin:0 0 6px">Total in this folder: <b>${fmt(data.total)}</b> · ${data.items.length} items</div>
    <table>${rows || "<tr><td>Empty folder</td></tr>"}</table>
    ${data.errors ? `<div class="note">⚠ ${data.errors} items could not be read (no permission). Run with sudo to include them.</div>` : ""}`;
  $("listing").querySelectorAll("tr.clickable").forEach(tr =>
    tr.addEventListener("click", () => openDir(tr.dataset.p)));
}

$("goBtn").onclick = () => openDir($("pathInput").value.trim());
$("pathInput").onkeydown = e => { if (e.key === "Enter") openDir($("pathInput").value.trim()); };
$("upBtn").onclick = () => current && current.parent && openDir(current.parent);
$("refreshBtn").onclick = () => current && openDir(current.path, true);

loadDisks();
openDir("");
</script>
</body>
</html>
"""


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Disk usage viewer with a web interface")
    ap.add_argument("--host", default="127.0.0.1", help="address to listen on (default: local only)")
    ap.add_argument("--port", type=int, default=8765)
    args = ap.parse_args()
    server = ThreadingHTTPServer((args.host, args.port), Handler)
    print(f"Disk Usage Viewer running at http://{args.host}:{args.port}  (Ctrl+C to stop)")
    try:
        server.serve_forever()
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        print("\nStopped.")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
