#!/usr/bin/env python3
# SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-or-later
# Copyright (C) 2026 Denis
"""Wired Network (LAN) - a tiny local web app that shows your wired connection
and the other devices on your home network.

Run:   python3 lanscan.py            then open http://127.0.0.1:8768
Opts:  --port 9000   --host 127.0.0.1

Uses standard Fedora tools (`ip`, `ping`, `nmcli`, `avahi-resolve`) and Python's
standard library. Device discovery sends one ping to each address on your own
local network - only scan networks you're allowed to (your home network is fine).
"""

import argparse
import ipaddress
import json
import os
import re
import socket
import subprocess
import time
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer
from urllib.parse import parse_qs, urlparse

OUI_FILE = "/usr/share/hwdata/oui.txt"
_oui = None
_names = {}  # ip -> name cache (names rarely change)

# Guess a device type from its maker's name: (keywords, emoji, kind)
VENDOR_HINTS = [
    (["apple"], "🍏", "Apple device"),
    (["raspberry"], "🍓", "Raspberry Pi"),
    (["sonos", "bose", "harman", "denon", "yamaha"], "🔊", "Speaker / audio"),
    (["amazon"], "🔊", "Amazon device (Echo / Fire TV)"),
    (["google", "nest"], "🏠", "Google / Nest device"),
    (["hewlett", "hp inc", "canon", "epson", "brother", "kyocera", "xerox", "lexmark"], "🖨️", "Printer"),
    (["philips", "signify", "lifx", "yeelight"], "💡", "Smart light"),
    (["espressif", "tuya", "shelly", "sonoff", "itead"], "🏠", "Smart home gadget"),
    (["ring"], "🔔", "Doorbell / camera"),
    (["synology", "qnap", "western digital", "seagate"], "🗄️", "Network storage"),
    (["nintendo", "sony interactive", "microsoft"], "🎮", "Games console / PC"),
    (["sky", "roku", "lg electronics", "tcl", "hisense", "vizio", "panasonic"], "📺", "TV / set-top box"),
    (["samsung"], "📱", "Samsung device"),
    (["tp-link", "netgear", "ubiquiti", "avm", "asustek", "linksys", "d-link", "zyxel",
      "mikrotik", "huawei", "arris", "technicolor", "sagemcom", "check point", "cisco",
      "eero", "plume"], "📡", "Network equipment"),
    (["intel", "realtek", "dell", "lenovo", "micro-star", "gigabyte", "asrock", "framework"], "💻", "Computer"),
]


def run(cmd, timeout=10):
    try:
        return subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=timeout).stdout
    except (subprocess.TimeoutExpired, FileNotFoundError):
        return ""


def read_sys(iface, name, default=None):
    try:
        with open(f"/sys/class/net/{iface}/{name}") as f:
            return f.read().strip()
    except OSError:
        return default


def load_oui():
    global _oui
    if _oui is None:
        _oui = {}
        try:
            with open(OUI_FILE, encoding="utf-8", errors="replace") as f:
                for line in f:
                    m = re.match(r"^([0-9A-F]{6})\s+\(base 16\)\s+(.+)$", line.strip())
                    if m:
                        _oui[m.group(1)] = m.group(2).strip()
        except OSError:
            pass
    return _oui


def vendor_for(mac):
    if not mac:
        return None, False
    first = int(mac[:2], 16)
    if first & 0x02:  # "locally administered" bit = randomised / private address
        return None, True
    return load_oui().get(mac.replace(":", "").upper()[:6]), False


def wired_interfaces():
    """Physical ethernet interfaces (not Wi-Fi, not virtual)."""
    ifaces = []
    for name in sorted(os.listdir("/sys/class/net")):
        if name == "lo" or not os.path.exists(f"/sys/class/net/{name}/device"):
            continue
        if os.path.exists(f"/sys/class/net/{name}/wireless") or read_sys(name, "type") != "1":
            continue
        ifaces.append(name)
    return ifaces


def connection_info():
    addrs = json.loads(run(["ip", "-j", "addr"]) or "[]")
    routes = json.loads(run(["ip", "-j", "route"]) or "[]")
    result = []
    for iface in wired_interfaces():
        a = next((x for x in addrs if x["ifname"] == iface), {})
        ipv4 = next((i for i in a.get("addr_info", []) if i["family"] == "inet"), None)
        ipv6 = [i["local"] for i in a.get("addr_info", []) if i["family"] == "inet6"]
        gateway = next((r.get("gateway") for r in routes
                        if r.get("dst") == "default" and r.get("dev") == iface), None)

        nm = {}
        for line in run(["nmcli", "-t", "dev", "show", iface]).splitlines():
            k, _, v = line.partition(":")
            nm[k] = v
        dns = [v for k, v in nm.items() if k.startswith("IP4.DNS")]

        carrier = read_sys(iface, "carrier") == "1"
        speed = read_sys(iface, "speed")
        try:
            speed = int(speed) if speed and int(speed) > 0 else None
        except ValueError:
            speed = None

        result.append({
            "iface": iface,
            "connection": nm.get("GENERAL.CONNECTION") or None,
            "plugged_in": carrier,
            "speed_mbps": speed if carrier else None,
            "duplex": read_sys(iface, "duplex") if carrier else None,
            "mac": a.get("address"),
            "ipv4": f"{ipv4['local']}/{ipv4['prefixlen']}" if ipv4 else None,
            "dhcp": bool(ipv4 and ipv4.get("dynamic")),
            "ipv6": ipv6,
            "gateway": gateway,
            "dns": dns,
        })
    return result


def ping(host, count=1, wait=1):
    out = run(["ping", "-n", "-q", "-c", str(count), "-W", str(wait), host], timeout=count * wait + 3)
    m = re.search(r"= [\d.]+/([\d.]+)/", out)
    return float(m.group(1)) if m else None


def internet_check(gateway):
    with ThreadPoolExecutor(3) as ex:
        gw = ex.submit(ping, gateway, 2) if gateway else None
        net = ex.submit(ping, "1.1.1.1", 2)

        def dns_ok():
            try:
                socket.getaddrinfo("fedoraproject.org", 443)
                return True
            except OSError:
                return False
        dns = ex.submit(dns_ok)
        return {
            "router_ms": gw.result() if gw else None,
            "internet_ms": net.result(),
            "dns_ok": dns.result(),
        }


def lookup_name(ip):
    if ip in _names:
        return _names[ip]
    name = None
    out = run(["avahi-resolve", "-a", ip], timeout=3).split()
    if len(out) >= 2:
        name = out[1]
    if not name:
        out = run(["getent", "hosts", ip], timeout=3).split()
        if len(out) >= 2 and out[1] not in ("_gateway",):
            name = out[1]
    if name:
        name = re.sub(r"\.(local|lan|home|localdomain)$", "", name)
        _names[ip] = name
    return name


def guess_kind(vendor, private, is_gateway, is_self):
    if is_self:
        return "🖥️", "This computer"
    if is_gateway:
        return "📡", "Router (your gateway to the internet)"
    if private:
        return "📱", "Phone, tablet or laptop (private address)"
    v = (vendor or "").lower()
    for words, emoji, kind in VENDOR_HINTS:
        if any(w in v for w in words):
            return emoji, kind
    return "✨", "Network device"


def scan_devices(conn):
    if not conn.get("ipv4") or not conn["plugged_in"]:
        return []
    iface = conn["iface"]
    me = ipaddress.ip_interface(conn["ipv4"])
    net = me.network
    if net.num_addresses > 1024:  # very big network: just scan the /24 around us
        net = ipaddress.ip_network(f"{me.ip}/24", strict=False)
    hosts = [str(h) for h in net.hosts() if h != me.ip]

    # One quick ping to every address; replies (and ARP answers) fill the neighbour table.
    latency = {}
    with ThreadPoolExecutor(128) as ex:
        for ip, ms in zip(hosts, ex.map(lambda h: ping(h, 1, 1), hosts)):
            if ms is not None:
                latency[ip] = ms

    found = {}
    for n in json.loads(run(["ip", "-j", "-4", "neigh", "show", "dev", iface]) or "[]"):
        state = n.get("state", [])
        if n.get("lladdr") and not set(state) & {"FAILED", "INCOMPLETE"}:
            found[n["dst"]] = n["lladdr"]
    for ip in latency:
        found.setdefault(ip, None)
    found[str(me.ip)] = conn["mac"]

    ips = sorted(found, key=lambda x: ipaddress.ip_address(x))
    with ThreadPoolExecutor(32) as ex:
        names = dict(zip(ips, ex.map(lookup_name, ips)))

    devices = []
    for ip in ips:
        mac = found[ip]
        vendor, private = vendor_for(mac)
        is_self = ip == str(me.ip)
        is_gw = ip == conn.get("gateway")
        emoji, kind = guess_kind(vendor, private, is_gw, is_self)
        devices.append({
            "ip": ip, "mac": mac, "vendor": vendor, "private_mac": private,
            "name": socket.gethostname() if is_self else names.get(ip),
            "emoji": emoji, "kind": kind, "is_self": is_self, "is_gateway": is_gw,
            "ping_ms": 0 if is_self else latency.get(ip),
        })
    devices.sort(key=lambda d: (not d["is_self"], not d["is_gateway"], ipaddress.ip_address(d["ip"])))
    return devices


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Port checker
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
import errno
import ssl
import threading
import uuid

# port -> (name, plain-English description, level)
# level: "web" = normal for a web server, "ok" = common & fine if you use it,
#        "check" = make sure you meant to open this, "risky" = usually should NOT be reachable
PORT_INFO = {
    20: ("FTP data", "Old-style file transfer (data channel).", "risky"),
    21: ("FTP", "Old file transfer. Passwords are sent unencrypted; prefer SFTP.", "risky"),
    22: ("SSH", "Remote login to the server. Fine if you use it; prefer key login over passwords.", "ok"),
    23: ("Telnet", "Ancient remote login with NO encryption. Should be closed.", "risky"),
    25: ("SMTP", "Mail server (sending email).", "check"),
    53: ("DNS", "Name server: turns names into addresses.", "check"),
    67: ("DHCP", "Hands out IP addresses.", "check"),
    69: ("TFTP", "Very simple file transfer with no passwords.", "risky"),
    80: ("HTTP", "Normal website (not encrypted). Expected on a web server.", "web"),
    81: ("HTTP (alt)", "Alternative website port.", "web"),
    88: ("Kerberos", "Windows / domain login service.", "check"),
    110: ("POP3", "Old mailbox access (unencrypted).", "check"),
    111: ("RPC", "Unix remote procedure calls. Rarely needed on the network.", "risky"),
    123: ("NTP", "Time synchronisation.", "ok"),
    135: ("MS RPC", "Windows internal service. Shouldn't be reachable from outside.", "risky"),
    137: ("NetBIOS", "Old Windows file-sharing name service.", "risky"),
    139: ("NetBIOS / SMB", "Old Windows file sharing.", "risky"),
    143: ("IMAP", "Mailbox access (unencrypted).", "check"),
    161: ("SNMP", "Device monitoring. Often uses weak default passwords.", "risky"),
    389: ("LDAP", "Directory / user database.", "check"),
    443: ("HTTPS", "Secure (encrypted) website. Expected on a web server.", "web"),
    445: ("SMB", "Windows / Samba file sharing. Never expose to the internet.", "risky"),
    465: ("SMTPS", "Encrypted mail sending.", "check"),
    514: ("Syslog / rsh", "Logging or old remote shell.", "risky"),
    548: ("AFP", "Apple file sharing.", "check"),
    587: ("SMTP submission", "Mail sending for email clients.", "check"),
    631: ("IPP / CUPS", "Printing service.", "check"),
    636: ("LDAPS", "Encrypted directory service.", "check"),
    873: ("rsync", "File sync. Make sure it requires a password.", "check"),
    993: ("IMAPS", "Encrypted mailbox access.", "ok"),
    995: ("POP3S", "Encrypted mailbox access.", "ok"),
    1080: ("SOCKS proxy", "Proxy server. Open proxies get abused.", "risky"),

    3306: ("MySQL / MariaDB", "Database. Usually should only listen on localhost.", "risky"),

    7001: ("WebLogic", "Java application server.", "check"),
    8000: ("HTTP (alt)", "Alternative website port, e.g. Django / Python dev server.", "web"),

    9200: ("Elasticsearch", "Search database, often with NO password. Should not be exposed.", "risky"),

    10000: ("Webmin", "Server admin panel. Protect carefully.", "check"),
    11211: ("Memcached", "Cache with no password. Should not be exposed.", "risky"),
    27017: ("MongoDB", "Database. Should not be exposed without a password.", "risky"),

TLS_FIRST = {443, 465, 636, 993, 995, 2376, 4443, 6443, 8443, 9443}

PRESETS = {
    "web": [80, 81, 443, 3000, 4443, 5000, 5173, 8000, 8008, 8080, 8081, 8088, 8443, 8888, 9000, 9090, 9443],
    "common": sorted(PORT_INFO),
    "top1024": list(range(1, 1025)),
    "all": list(range(1, 65536)),
}

_jobs = {}
_jobs_lock = threading.Lock()


def parse_ports(spec):
    spec = (spec or "").strip().lower()
    if spec in PRESETS:
        return PRESETS[spec]
    ports = set()
    for part in re.split(r"[,\s]+", spec):
        if not part:
            continue
        m = re.fullmatch(r"(\d+)(?:-(\d+))?", part)
        if not m:
            raise ValueError(f"'{part}' is not a port or range (example: 22,80,8000-8100)")
        a, b = int(m.group(1)), int(m.group(2) or m.group(1))
        if a > b:
            a, b = b, a
        if a < 1 or b > 65535:
            raise ValueError("Ports must be between 1 and 65535")
        ports.update(range(a, b + 1))
    if not ports:
        raise ValueError("Please choose some ports to check")
    return sorted(ports)


def _parse_http(data):
    text = data.decode("latin-1", "replace")
    if not text.startswith("HTTP/"):
        return None
    lines = text.split("\r\n")
    status = lines[0].split(" ", 1)[1] if " " in lines[0] else lines[0]
    server = next((l.split(":", 1)[1].strip() for l in lines if l.lower().startswith("server:")), None)
    return status.strip(), server


def identify(ip, port, hostname):
    """Find out what's listening: a banner (SSH, FTP...), a website, or a secure website."""
    request = f"HEAD / HTTP/1.0\r\nHost: {hostname}\r\nUser-Agent: lan-port-check\r\n\r\n".encode()

    def plain():
        with socket.create_connection((ip, port), timeout=2) as s:
            s.settimeout(1.2)
            try:
                first = s.recv(256)  # services that talk first (SSH, FTP, SMTP, MySQL...)
            except socket.timeout:
                first = b""
            if first:
                if first[:1] in (b"\x15", b"\x16"):
                    return None  # TLS alert/handshake -> it's an encrypted service
                line = first.split(b"\n")[0].decode("latin-1", "replace").strip()
                line = "".join(ch for ch in line if ch.isprintable())[:120]
                return {"kind": "banner", "text": line} if line else None
            s.settimeout(2)
            s.sendall(request)
            data = s.recv(2048)
            http = _parse_http(data)
            if http:
                return {"kind": "http", "status": http[0], "server": http[1]}
            if data[:1] in (b"\x15", b"\x16"):
                return None
            return {"kind": "unknown"} if data else None

    def tls():
        ctx = ssl.create_default_context()
        ctx.check_hostname = False
        ctx.verify_mode = ssl.CERT_NONE  # we only want to look, not trust
        with socket.create_connection((ip, port), timeout=2) as raw:
            with ctx.wrap_socket(raw, server_hostname=hostname if not _is_ip(hostname) else None) as s:
                s.settimeout(2)
                info = {"kind": "tls", "tls": s.version()}
                try:
                    s.sendall(request)
                    http = _parse_http(s.recv(2048))
                    if http:
                        info.update(kind="https", status=http[0], server=http[1])
                except (OSError, ssl.SSLError):
                    pass
                return info

    order = (tls, plain) if port in TLS_FIRST else (plain, tls)
    for probe in order:
        try:
            result = probe()
            if result:
                return result
        except (OSError, ssl.SSLError):
            continue
    return None


def _is_ip(s):
    try:
        ipaddress.ip_address(s)
        return True
    except ValueError:
        return False


def check_port(ip, port, timeout):
    family = socket.AF_INET6 if ":" in ip else socket.AF_INET
    with socket.socket(family, socket.SOCK_STREAM) as s:
        s.settimeout(timeout)
        try:
            code = s.connect_ex((ip, port))
        except socket.timeout:
            return "filtered"
        except OSError:
            return "filtered"
    if code == 0:
        return "open"
    if code == errno.ECONNREFUSED:
        return "closed"
    return "filtered"  # timed out / unreachable: a firewall is silently dropping it


def start_port_job(host, spec, timeout=1.0):
    host = host.strip()
    if not host or not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9.\-:\[\]]+", host):
        raise ValueError("Please enter a server name or IP address, like 192.168.1.20 or myserver.local")
    host = host.strip("[]")
    ports = parse_ports(spec)
    try:
        infos = socket.getaddrinfo(host, None, proto=socket.IPPROTO_TCP)
        # Prefer IPv4: most home/test servers listen there.
        infos.sort(key=lambda i: i[0] != socket.AF_INET)
        ip = infos[0][4][0]
    except (socket.gaierror, UnicodeError):
        raise ValueError(f"Couldn't find a server called '{host}'. Check the spelling or use its IP address.")

    job = {"id": uuid.uuid4().hex[:12], "host": host, "ip": ip, "total": len(ports),
           "done": 0, "open": [], "closed": 0, "filtered": 0, "finished": False,
           "cancel": False, "started": time.time(), "elapsed": 0}
    with _jobs_lock:
        for jid in [j for j, v in _jobs.items() if v["finished"] and time.time() - v["started"] > 3600]:
            del _jobs[jid]
        _jobs[job["id"]] = job

    def worker(port):
        if job["cancel"]:
            return
        state = check_port(ip, port, timeout)
        entry = None
        if state == "open":
            name, desc, level = PORT_INFO.get(port, (None, None, "check"))
            if not name:
                try:
                    name = socket.getservbyport(port, "tcp").upper()
                except OSError:
                    name = "Unknown service"
                desc = "Not a well-known port. Make sure you know what's running here."
            found = identify(ip, port, host)
            if port not in PORT_INFO and found and found["kind"] in ("http", "https"):
                name = "Website (HTTPS)" if found["kind"] == "https" else "Website (HTTP)"
                desc = "A web server is answering on this port."
                level = "web"
            entry = {"port": port, "name": name, "desc": desc, "level": level, "found": found}
        with _jobs_lock:
            job["done"] += 1
            if entry:
                job["open"].append(entry)
            elif state == "closed":
                job["closed"] += 1
            elif state == "filtered":
                job["filtered"] += 1

    def run_job():
        with ThreadPoolExecutor(min(256, len(ports))) as ex:
            list(ex.map(worker, ports))
        with _jobs_lock:
            job["open"].sort(key=lambda e: e["port"])
            job["finished"] = True
            job["elapsed"] = round(time.time() - job["started"], 1)

    threading.Thread(target=run_job, daemon=True).start()
    return job["id"]


def job_status(jid):
    with _jobs_lock:
        job = _jobs.get(jid)
        if not job:
            raise ValueError("That scan was not found (maybe the app was restarted).")
        out = dict(job, open=sorted(job["open"], key=lambda e: e["port"]))
        if not job["finished"]:
            out["elapsed"] = round(time.time() - job["started"], 1)
        return out


def traffic():
    data = {"time": time.time(), "ifaces": {}}
    for iface in wired_interfaces():
        data["ifaces"][iface] = {
            "rx": int(read_sys(iface, "statistics/rx_bytes", "0")),
            "tx": int(read_sys(iface, "statistics/tx_bytes", "0")),
        }
    return data


class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
    def _send(self, code, body, ctype="application/json"):
        data = body.encode() if isinstance(body, str) else body
        self.send_response(code)
        self.send_header("Content-Type", ctype)
        self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(data)))
        self.end_headers()
        self.wfile.write(data)

    def do_GET(self):
        url = urlparse(self.path)
        try:
            if url.path == "/":
                self._send(200, PAGE, "text/html; charset=utf-8")
            elif url.path == "/api/connection":
                conns = connection_info()
                main = next((c for c in conns if c["plugged_in"] and c["ipv4"]), None)
                health = internet_check(main["gateway"]) if main else None
                self._send(200, json.dumps({"connections": conns, "health": health,
                                            "hostname": socket.gethostname()}))
            elif url.path == "/api/devices":
                conns = connection_info()
                main = next((c for c in conns if c["plugged_in"] and c["ipv4"]), None)
                devs = scan_devices(main) if main else []
                self._send(200, json.dumps({"devices": devs,
                                            "network": main["ipv4"] if main else None}))
            elif url.path == "/api/traffic":
                self._send(200, json.dumps(traffic()))
            elif url.path == "/api/ports/start":
                q = parse_qs(url.query)
                try:
                    jid = start_port_job(q.get("host", [""])[0], q.get("ports", ["web"])[0])
                except ValueError as e:
                    self._send(400, json.dumps({"error": str(e)}))
                    return
                self._send(200, json.dumps({"id": jid}))
            elif url.path == "/api/ports/status":
                q = parse_qs(url.query)
                try:
                    self._send(200, json.dumps(job_status(q.get("id", [""])[0])))
                except ValueError as e:
                    self._send(404, json.dumps({"error": str(e)}))
            elif url.path == "/api/ports/cancel":
                q = parse_qs(url.query)
                with _jobs_lock:
                    job = _jobs.get(q.get("id", [""])[0])
                    if job:
                        job["cancel"] = True
                self._send(200, json.dumps({"ok": True}))
            else:
                self._send(404, json.dumps({"error": "not found"}))
        except Exception as e:
            self._send(500, json.dumps({"error": str(e)}))

    def log_message(self, fmt, *args):
        pass


PAGE = r"""<!doctype html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
<meta charset="utf-8">
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">
<title>Wired Network</title>
<link rel="icon" href="data:image/svg+xml,<svg xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg' viewBox='0 0 100 100'><text y='.9em' font-size='90'>🔌</text></svg>">
<style>
  :root {
    --sun: #ffd23f; --coral: #ff6b6b; --mint: #3ddc97; --sky: #4cc9f0; --teal: #06b6a4;
    --grape: #9b5de5; --pink: #f15bb5; --orange: #ff9f1c; --ink: #2b2d42; --soft: #6c6f85;
    --card: #ffffff;
  }
  * { box-sizing: border-box; }
  body {
    margin: 0; min-height: 100vh; color: var(--ink);
    font: 15px/1.5 "Nunito", system-ui, -apple-system, "Segoe UI", sans-serif;
    background: linear-gradient(135deg, #d8fbef 0%, #fff4cc 50%, #ffe0d1 100%) fixed;
  }

  <div class="card ports">
    <p class="sub" style="margin-top:0">A "port" is like a numbered door on a server. Each program that accepts
      connections (website, SSH, database…) waits behind its own door. Let's see which doors are open!</p>
    <div class="pform">
      <input id="pHost" placeholder="Server address, e.g. 192.168.201.50 or myserver.local" spellcheck="false">

  <div class="legend">
    <h2>🌈 How to read this</h2>
    <ul>
      <li><b>Link speed</b> is the speed of your cable connection, for example 1000 Mbit/s (= 1 Gigabit). Your internet plan is often slower than this.</li>
      <li><b>IP address</b> is your computer's "house number" on the home network. The <b>router</b> is the box that connects your home to the internet.</li>
      <li><b>Response time</b> (ms) is how quickly a device answers. Under 5 ms is excellent for devices at home. Under 50 ms is great for the internet.</li>
      <li><b>Maker</b> is worked out from the device's hardware address. <b>Private address</b> means a phone or laptop is hiding its real identity, which is normal.</li>
      <li>Some devices don't answer pings or are asleep, so they may not appear every time. Try scanning again.</li>
      <li><b>Ports:</b> 🟢 <b>Open</b> means a program is answering. 🔒 <b>Closed</b> means the server said "nobody here".
        🧱 <b>Blocked</b> means a firewall silently ignored us, which is good for ports you don't use.
        Colours on open ports: 🌐 normal for a web server, 👍 fine if you use it, 🤔 check you meant this, 🚨 usually shouldn't be reachable.</li>
    </ul>
  </div>
  <p class="foot">Live traffic updates every 2 seconds · connection check every 60 seconds · <span id="updated"></span></p>

<script>
const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);
const esc = s => String(s ?? "").replace(/[&<>"]/g, c => ({"&":"&amp;","<":"&lt;",">":"&gt;",'"':"&quot;"}[c]));

function fmtBytes(b) {
  const u = ["B", "KB", "MB", "GB", "TB"]; let i = 0;
  while (b >= 1024 && i < u.length - 1) { b /= 1024; i++; }
  return (i ? b.toFixed(b < 10 ? 1 : 0) : b) + " " + u[i];
}
function fmtRate(bytesPerSec) {
  const bits = bytesPerSec * 8;
  if (bits >= 1e9) return (bits / 1e9).toFixed(2) + " Gbit/s";
  if (bits >= 1e6) return (bits / 1e6).toFixed(1) + " Mbit/s";
  if (bits >= 1e3) return (bits / 1e3).toFixed(0) + " kbit/s";
  return bits.toFixed(0) + " bit/s";
}
function speedWords(mbps) {
  if (!mbps) return "";
  if (mbps >= 10000) return "Super fast 🚀 (" + mbps / 1000 + " Gigabit)";
  if (mbps >= 2500) return "Super fast 🚀 (" + mbps / 1000 + " Gigabit)";
  if (mbps >= 1000) return "Fast 🏎️ (1 Gigabit)";
  if (mbps >= 100) return "Okay 🚗. Check the cable if you expected Gigabit";
  return "Slow 🐢. Try another cable or port";
}
function latencyLook(ms, home) {
  if (ms == null) return { t: "No answer", e: "😟", bg: "#ffe1e1" };
  const good = home ? 5 : 50, ok = home ? 20 : 120;
  if (ms <= good) return { t: ms.toFixed(1) + " ms · Excellent", e: "🤩", bg: "#dcfaea" };
  if (ms <= ok) return { t: ms.toFixed(1) + " ms · Good", e: "😊", bg: "#ddf5fd" };
  return { t: ms.toFixed(0) + " ms · Slow", e: "🙂", bg: "#fff6cf" };
}

async function loadConnection() {
  $("checkBtn").disabled = true;
  try {
    const res = await fetch("/api/connection");
    const data = await res.json();
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(data.error);
    renderConnection(data);
    $("updated").textContent = "Last check " + new Date().toLocaleTimeString();
  } catch (e) {
    $("conn").innerHTML = `<div class="msg err">😬 ${esc(e.message)}</div>`;
  } finally { $("checkBtn").disabled = false; }
}

function renderConnection(data) {
  const conns = data.connections;
  if (!conns.length) {
    $("conn").innerHTML = `<div class="card hero" style="--c:var(--coral)"><div class="big">🤷</div>
      <div><div class="title">No wired network port found</div><div class="sub">This computer doesn't seem to have an Ethernet port.</div></div></div>`;
    return;
  }
  const c = conns.find(x => x.plugged_in && x.ipv4) || conns.find(x => x.plugged_in) || conns[0];
  const h = data.health;
  let hero;
  if (!c.plugged_in) {
    hero = { c: "var(--coral)", e: "🔌", t: "Cable not plugged in", s: `Plug a network cable into <b>${esc(c.iface)}</b> to get connected.` };
  } else if (!c.ipv4) {
    hero = { c: "var(--sun)", e: "⏳", t: "Cable connected, waiting for an address", s: "Your router hasn't given this computer an IP address yet." };
  } else if (h && h.internet_ms != null && h.dns_ok) {
    hero = { c: "var(--mint)", e: "🎉", t: "You're online through the cable!", s: `Everything looks healthy on <b>${esc(c.connection || c.iface)}</b>.` };
  } else if (h && h.router_ms != null) {
    hero = { c: "var(--orange)", e: "🤔", t: "Connected to your router, but the internet isn't answering", s: "Try restarting the router, or check whether your internet provider has a problem." };
  } else {
    hero = { c: "var(--coral)", e: "😟", t: "Cable connected, but the router isn't answering", s: "Try unplugging the cable and plugging it back in." };
  }

  const checks = h ? [
    ["🔌 Cable", c.plugged_in ? "Plugged in" : "Unplugged", c.plugged_in ? "#dcfaea" : "#ffe1e1", ""],
    (h.router_ms == null && h.internet_ms != null)
      ? ["📡 Router", "🤫 Doesn't answer pings", "#ddf5fd", "That's fine, your internet works through it"]
      : ["📡 Router", latencyLook(h.router_ms, true).e + " " + latencyLook(h.router_ms, true).t, latencyLook(h.router_ms, true).bg, "How fast your router answers"],
    ["🌍 Internet", latencyLook(h.internet_ms, false).e + " " + latencyLook(h.internet_ms, false).t, latencyLook(h.internet_ms, false).bg, "Time to reach a server on the internet"],
    ["📖 Website names", h.dns_ok ? "😊 Working" : "😟 Not working", h.dns_ok ? "#dcfaea" : "#ffe1e1", "Turning names like fedoraproject.org into addresses"],
  ] : [];

  const facts = [
    ["⚡ Link speed", c.speed_mbps ? c.speed_mbps + " Mbit/s" : "–", speedWords(c.speed_mbps) + (c.duplex ? ` · ${c.duplex} duplex` : "")],
    ["🏡 Your IP address", c.ipv4 || "–", c.dhcp ? "Given automatically by your router (DHCP)" : "Set manually"],
    ["📡 Router address", c.gateway || "–", "Your gateway to the internet"],
    ["📖 DNS server", c.dns.join(", ") || "–", "Looks up website names for you"],
    ["🏷️ Computer name", data.hostname, "How this PC calls itself"],
    ["🔖 Hardware address", c.mac || "–", `Network port ${esc(c.iface)}`],
  ];

  $("conn").innerHTML = `
    <div class="card hero" style="--c:${hero.c}">
      <div class="big">${hero.e}</div>
      <div><div class="title">${hero.t}</div><div class="sub">${hero.s}</div></div>
    </div>
    <div class="checks">${checks.map(([t, v, bg, d]) =>
      `<div class="check" style="--bg:${bg}"><div class="t">${t}</div><div>${v}</div><div class="d">${d}</div></div>`).join("")}</div>
    <div class="facts">${facts.map(([k, v, e]) =>
      `<div class="fact"><div class="k">${k}</div><div class="v">${esc(v)}</div><div class="e">${e}</div></div>`).join("")}</div>`;
}

// ---- live traffic ----
let prev = null; const hist = { rx: [], tx: [] };
function spark(id, arr, color) {
  const max = Math.max(...arr, 1), n = 30;
  const pts = arr.map((v, i) => `${(i + n - arr.length) / (n - 1) * 100},${28 - v / max * 26}`).join(" ");
  $(id).innerHTML = arr.length > 1
    ? `<polyline points="${pts}" fill="none" stroke="${color}" stroke-width="1.5" vector-effect="non-scaling-stroke" stroke-linejoin="round"/>`
    : "";
}
async function pollTraffic() {
  try {
    const d = await (await fetch("/api/traffic")).json();
    const sum = k => Object.values(d.ifaces).reduce((s, x) => s + x[k], 0);
    const cur = { t: d.time, rx: sum("rx"), tx: sum("tx") };
    if (prev) {
      const dt = cur.t - prev.t;
      const rx = Math.max(0, (cur.rx - prev.rx) / dt), tx = Math.max(0, (cur.tx - prev.tx) / dt);
      hist.rx.push(rx); hist.tx.push(tx);
      if (hist.rx.length > 30) { hist.rx.shift(); hist.tx.shift(); }
      $("rxRate").textContent = fmtRate(rx); $("txRate").textContent = fmtRate(tx);
      spark("rxSpark", hist.rx, "#06b6a4"); spark("txSpark", hist.tx, "#ff6b6b");
    }
    $("rxTot").textContent = fmtBytes(cur.rx) + " received since start-up";
    $("txTot").textContent = fmtBytes(cur.tx) + " sent since start-up";
    prev = cur;
  } catch (e) {}
}

// ---- devices ----
async function scanDevices() {
  $("scanBtn").disabled = true;
  $("scanBtn").innerHTML = `<span class="spin">🔍</span> Looking…`;
  $("devices").innerHTML = `<div class="msg">📣 Saying hello to every address on your network…</div>`;
  const start = Date.now();
  const timer = setInterval(() => { $("bar").style.width = Math.min(95, (Date.now() - start) / 80) + "%"; }, 100);
  try {
    const res = await fetch("/api/devices");
    const data = await res.json();
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(data.error);
    renderDevices(data);
  } catch (e) {
    $("devices").innerHTML = `<div class="msg err">😬 ${esc(e.message)}</div>`;
  } finally {
    clearInterval(timer); $("bar").style.width = "0";
    $("scanBtn").disabled = false; $("scanBtn").textContent = "🔍 Find devices";
  }
}

function renderDevices(data) {
  const devs = data.devices;
  if (!devs.length) { $("devices").innerHTML = `<div class="msg">🙈 No devices found. Is the cable plugged in?</div>`; return; }
  const colors = ["var(--sky)", "var(--grape)", "var(--pink)", "var(--sun)", "var(--teal)", "var(--orange)"];
  const bgs = ["#ddf5fd", "#eee3ff", "#ffe0f1", "#fff6cf", "#d6f7f2", "#ffe8cc"];
  const others = devs.filter(d => !d.is_self && !d.is_gateway);
  const summary = `<div class="summary">
    <div class="pill a"><b>${devs.length}</b>devices on your network</div>
    <div class="pill b"><b>${others.filter(d => d.name).length}</b>with a name</div>
    <div class="pill c"><b>${others.filter(d => d.private_mac).length}</b>phones / laptops with private addresses</div>
    <div class="pill d"><b>${esc(data.network)}</b>your network</div></div>`;
  $("devices").innerHTML = summary + `<div class="grid">${devs.map((d, i) => {
    const col = d.is_self ? "var(--mint)" : d.is_gateway ? "var(--orange)" : colors[i % colors.length];
    const bg = d.is_self ? "#dcfaea" : d.is_gateway ? "#ffe8cc" : bgs[i % bgs.length];
    const title = d.name ? `<div class="name">${esc(d.name)}</div>`
      : `<div class="name unnamed">${d.vendor ? esc(d.vendor.split(/[ ,]/)[0]) + " device" : "Unknown device"}</div>`;
    const badges = [
      d.is_self ? `<span class="badge b-me">💚 This is you</span>` : "",
      d.is_gateway ? `<span class="badge b-gw">🌍 Router</span>` : "",
      `<span class="badge b-ip">🏡 ${esc(d.ip)}</span>`,
      d.vendor ? `<span class="badge b-vendor">🏭 ${esc(d.vendor)}</span>` : "",
      d.private_mac ? `<span class="badge b-vendor">🕶️ Private address</span>` : "",
      !d.is_self && d.ping_ms != null ? `<span class="badge b-ping">⏱️ ${d.ping_ms.toFixed(1)} ms</span>` : "",
      !d.is_self && d.ping_ms == null ? `<span class="badge b-ping">🤫 Doesn't answer pings</span>` : "",
    ].join("");
    return `<div class="dev" style="--c:${col};--bg:${bg}">
      <div class="row"><div class="emoji">${d.emoji}</div><div>${title}<div class="kind">${esc(d.kind)}</div></div></div>
      ${badges}
      <div class="mac">${esc(d.mac || "")}</div>
      <button class="mini" data-ip="${esc(d.ip)}">🚪 Check its ports</button></div>`;
  }).join("")}</div>`;
  document.querySelectorAll(".mini[data-ip]").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => {
    $("pHost").value = b.dataset.ip;
    $("pHost").scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth", block: "center" });
    $("pHost").focus();
  });
}

// ---- port checker ----
let preset = "web", jobId = null, pollTimer = null;
const LEVELS = {
  web:   { e: "🌐", t: "Normal for a web server", c: "var(--mint)", bg: "#dcfaea" },
  ok:    { e: "👍", t: "Fine if you use it", c: "var(--sky)", bg: "#ddf5fd" },
  check: { e: "🤔", t: "Check you meant to open this", c: "var(--sun)", bg: "#fff6cf" },
  risky: { e: "🚨", t: "Usually shouldn't be reachable", c: "var(--coral)", bg: "#ffe1e1" },
};

document.querySelectorAll("#presets .chip").forEach(c => c.onclick = () => {
  document.querySelectorAll("#presets .chip").forEach(x => x.classList.remove("on"));
  c.classList.add("on");
  preset = c.dataset.p;
  $("pCustom").hidden = preset !== "custom";
  if (preset === "custom") $("pCustom").focus();
});

function foundText(f) {
  if (!f) return "";
  if (f.kind === "http") return `Website answered: ${esc(f.status)}${f.server ? " · " + esc(f.server) : ""}`;
  if (f.kind === "https") return `🔐 Secure website (${esc(f.tls)}): ${esc(f.status)}${f.server ? " · " + esc(f.server) : ""}`;
  if (f.kind === "tls") return `🔐 Encrypted service (${esc(f.tls)})`;
  if (f.kind === "banner") return `Says: "${esc(f.text)}"`;
  return "";
}

function renderPorts(j) {
  const pct = j.total ? Math.round(j.done / j.total * 100) : 0;
  const risky = j.open.filter(p => p.level === "risky").length;
  let verdict = "";
  if (j.finished) {
    verdict = !j.open.length ? `😴 No open ports found among the ones we checked.`
      : risky ? `🚨 ${risky} open port${risky > 1 ? "s" : ""} that usually shouldn't be reachable. Have a look below.`
      : `🎉 Nothing scary open. Looks tidy!`;
  }
  $("pResult").innerHTML = `
    <div class="pbar"><span style="width:${pct}%"></span></div>
    <div class="quality" style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px">
      <span>${j.finished ? "✅ Done" : `<span class="spin">🌀</span> Checking`} ${esc(j.host)}${j.host !== j.ip ? ` (${esc(j.ip)})` : ""}
        · ${j.done.toLocaleString()} / ${j.total.toLocaleString()} ports · ${j.elapsed}s</span>
      ${j.finished ? "" : `<button class="mini" id="pCancel" style="margin:0;background:var(--coral)">✋ Stop</button>`}
    </div>
    <div class="summary" style="margin-top:14px">
      <div class="pill a"><b>${j.open.length}</b>🟢 open</div>
      <div class="pill c"><b>${j.closed.toLocaleString()}</b>🔒 closed</div>
      <div class="pill d"><b>${j.filtered.toLocaleString()}</b>🧱 blocked by firewall</div>
      <div class="pill b"><b>${risky}</b>🚨 worth a look</div>
    </div>
    ${verdict ? `<div class="status" style="margin:0">${verdict}</div>` : ""}
    <div class="plist">${j.open.map(p => {
      const L = LEVELS[p.level] || LEVELS.check;
      const f = foundText(p.found);
      return `<div class="port" style="--c:${L.c};--bg:${L.bg}">
        <div class="num">🚪 ${p.port} <span class="svc">· ${esc(p.name)}</span></div>
        <div><b>${L.e} ${L.t}</b></div>
        <div class="desc">${esc(p.desc)}</div>
        ${f ? `<div class="found">${f}</div>` : ""}
      </div>`;
    }).join("")}</div>`;
  const cancel = $("pCancel");
  if (cancel) cancel.onclick = () => fetch("/api/ports/cancel?id=" + jobId);
}

async function startPorts() {
  const host = $("pHost").value.trim();
  const spec = preset === "custom" ? $("pCustom").value.trim() : preset;
  if (!host) { $("pResult").innerHTML = `<div class="msg err">✍️ Type a server address first.</div>`; return; }
  clearInterval(pollTimer);
  $("pStart").disabled = true;
  try {
    const res = await fetch(`/api/ports/start?host=${encodeURIComponent(host)}&ports=${encodeURIComponent(spec)}`);
    const data = await res.json();
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(data.error);
    jobId = data.id;
    const poll = async () => {
      const r = await fetch("/api/ports/status?id=" + jobId);
      const j = await r.json();
      if (!r.ok) { clearInterval(pollTimer); $("pResult").innerHTML = `<div class="msg err">😬 ${esc(j.error)}</div>`; $("pStart").disabled = false; return; }
      renderPorts(j);
      if (j.finished) { clearInterval(pollTimer); $("pStart").disabled = false; }
    };
    pollTimer = setInterval(poll, 700);
    poll();
  } catch (e) {
    $("pResult").innerHTML = `<div class="msg err">😬 ${esc(e.message)}</div>`;
    $("pStart").disabled = false;
  }
}
$("pStart").onclick = startPorts;
$("pHost").onkeydown = e => { if (e.key === "Enter") startPorts(); };
$("pCustom").onkeydown = e => { if (e.key === "Enter") startPorts(); };

$("checkBtn").onclick = loadConnection;
$("scanBtn").onclick = scanDevices;
loadConnection();
pollTraffic(); setInterval(pollTraffic, 2000);
setInterval(loadConnection, 60000);
</script>
</body>
</html>
"""


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Show your wired network in a web page")
    ap.add_argument("--host", default="127.0.0.1", help="address to listen on (default: local only)")
    ap.add_argument("--port", type=int, default=8768)
    args = ap.parse_args()
    server = ThreadingHTTPServer((args.host, args.port), Handler)
    print(f"Wired Network running at http://{args.host}:{args.port}  (Ctrl+C to stop)")
    try:
        server.serve_forever()
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        print("\nStopped.")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

================================================================================
FILE: sysmon.py
================================================================================
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-or-later
# Copyright (C) 2026 Denis
"""SYSMON // a geeky live system monitor in your browser.

Run:   python3 sysmon.py            then open http://127.0.0.1:8769
Opts:  --port 9000   --host 127.0.0.1

Shows CPU (total, per core, frequency, temperature, load), RAM and swap,
storage ("ROM") space and disk activity, top processes and battery.
Reads everything straight from /proc and /sys - Python standard library only.
"""

import argparse
import json
import os
import platform
import socket
import threading
import time
from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer
from urllib.parse import urlparse

SAMPLE_EVERY = 1.0
CLK_TCK = os.sysconf("SC_CLK_TCK")
PAGE_SIZE = os.sysconf("SC_PAGE_SIZE")
PSEUDO_FS = {"proc", "sysfs", "devtmpfs", "devpts", "tmpfs", "securityfs", "cgroup", "cgroup2",
             "pstore", "bpf", "debugfs", "tracefs", "configfs", "fusectl", "mqueue", "hugetlbfs",
             "autofs", "efivarfs", "selinuxfs", "binfmt_misc", "rpc_pipefs", "nsfs", "ramfs",
             "squashfs", "overlay"}

_latest = {}
_lock = threading.Lock()


def read(path, default=""):
    try:
        with open(path) as f:
            return f.read()
    except OSError:
        return default


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- static info
def static_info():
    cpuinfo = read("/proc/cpuinfo")
    model = next((l.split(":", 1)[1].strip() for l in cpuinfo.splitlines()
                  if l.startswith("model name")), platform.processor() or "Unknown CPU")
    osname = next((l.split("=", 1)[1].strip().strip('"') for l in read("/etc/os-release").splitlines()
                   if l.startswith("PRETTY_NAME=")), platform.system())
    return {
        "hostname": socket.gethostname(),
        "os": osname,
        "kernel": platform.release(),
        "arch": platform.machine(),
        "cpu_model": " ".join(model.split()),
        "cores": os.cpu_count(),
    }


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- samplers
def cpu_times():
    """Per-CPU (busy, total) jiffies; index 0 = all CPUs."""
    out = []
    for line in read("/proc/stat").splitlines():
        if not line.startswith("cpu"):
            break
        v = [int(x) for x in line.split()[1:]]
        idle = v[3] + (v[4] if len(v) > 4 else 0)  # idle + iowait
        total = sum(v[:8])  # exclude guest (already counted in user)
        out.append((total - idle, total))
    return out


def cpu_freqs():
    freqs = []
    for i in range(os.cpu_count() or 1):
        f = read(f"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu{i}/cpufreq/scaling_cur_freq").strip()
        freqs.append(int(f) // 1000 if f.isdigit() else None)
    if all(f is None for f in freqs):
        freqs = [int(float(l.split(":")[1])) for l in read("/proc/cpuinfo").splitlines()
                 if l.startswith("cpu MHz")]
    mx = read("/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu0/cpufreq/cpuinfo_max_freq").strip()
    return freqs, (int(mx) // 1000 if mx.isdigit() else None)


def temperatures():
    temps = []
    base = "/sys/class/hwmon"
    try:
        hwmons = sorted(os.listdir(base))
    except OSError:
        return temps
    for h in hwmons:
        d = os.path.join(base, h)
        name = read(f"{d}/name").strip()
        if name not in ("coretemp", "k10temp", "zenpower", "acpitz", "nvme", "pch_cannonlake",
                        "amdgpu", "nouveau", "iwlwifi_1", "thinkpad") and not name.startswith("pch_"):
            continue
        for f in sorted(os.listdir(d)):
            if f.startswith("temp") and f.endswith("_input"):
                raw = read(f"{d}/{f}").strip()
                if not raw.lstrip("-").isdigit():
                    continue
                c = int(raw) / 1000
                if c <= 0 or c > 150:
                    continue
                label = read(f"{d}/{f.replace('_input', '_label')}").strip()
                if not label:
                    friendly = {"acpitz": "board", "nvme": "nvme", "amdgpu": "gpu", "nouveau": "gpu",
                                "iwlwifi_1": "wifi", "thinkpad": "laptop"}
                    label = friendly.get(name, "chipset" if name.startswith("pch_") else name)
                    n = f[4:-6]
                    if n != "1":
                        label += n
                temps.append({"sensor": name, "label": label, "c": round(c, 1)})
    return temps


def memory():
    m = {}
    for line in read("/proc/meminfo").splitlines():
        k, _, v = line.partition(":")
        m[k] = int(v.split()[0]) * 1024 if v.strip() else 0
    total = m.get("MemTotal", 0)
    avail = m.get("MemAvailable", m.get("MemFree", 0))
    cache = m.get("Cached", 0) + m.get("SReclaimable", 0) + m.get("Buffers", 0)
    return {
        "total": total,
        "used": total - avail,
        "available": avail,
        "cache": cache,
        "free": m.get("MemFree", 0),
        "swap_total": m.get("SwapTotal", 0),
        "swap_used": m.get("SwapTotal", 0) - m.get("SwapFree", 0),
    }


def filesystems():
    seen = {}
    for line in read("/proc/mounts").splitlines():
        device, mount, fstype, *_ = line.split()
        mount = mount.replace("\\040", " ")
        if fstype in PSEUDO_FS or not device.startswith("/"):
            continue
        try:
            st = os.statvfs(mount)
        except OSError:
            continue
        total = st.f_blocks * st.f_frsize
        if not total:
            continue
        key = (device, total)
        if key in seen:
            seen[key]["mounts"].append(mount)
            continue
        seen[key] = {"device": device.split("/")[-1], "fstype": fstype, "mounts": [mount],
                     "total": total, "used": (st.f_blocks - st.f_bfree) * st.f_frsize,
                     "free": st.f_bavail * st.f_frsize}
    return sorted(seen.values(), key=lambda x: -x["total"])


def disk_counters():
    """Bytes read/written per physical disk (not partitions, zram or loop)."""
    disks = {}
    physical = {d for d in os.listdir("/sys/block")
                if not d.startswith(("loop", "zram", "ram", "dm-", "sr", "md"))}
    for line in read("/proc/diskstats").splitlines():
        p = line.split()
        if len(p) > 9 and p[2] in physical:
            disks[p[2]] = (int(p[5]) * 512, int(p[9]) * 512)
    return disks


def processes():
    procs = {}
    for pid in os.listdir("/proc"):
        if not pid.isdigit():
            continue
        stat = read(f"/proc/{pid}/stat")
        if not stat:
            continue
        try:
            name = stat[stat.index("(") + 1:stat.rindex(")")]
            rest = stat[stat.rindex(")") + 2:].split()
            ticks = int(rest[11]) + int(rest[12])  # utime + stime
            rss = int(rest[21]) * PAGE_SIZE
            state = rest[0]
        except (ValueError, IndexError):
            continue
        procs[int(pid)] = (name, ticks, rss, state)
    return procs


def users_of(pid):
    try:
        uid = os.stat(f"/proc/{pid}").st_uid
        import pwd
        return pwd.getpwuid(uid).pw_name
    except (OSError, KeyError):
        return "?"


def battery():
    base = "/sys/class/power_supply"
    try:
        names = os.listdir(base)
    except OSError:
        return None
    for n in names:
        if read(f"{base}/{n}/type").strip() == "Battery" and read(f"{base}/{n}/scope").strip() != "Device":
            cap = read(f"{base}/{n}/capacity").strip()
            if cap.isdigit():
                return {"percent": int(cap), "status": read(f"{base}/{n}/status").strip()}
    return None


def sampler():
    prev_cpu = cpu_times()
    prev_disk = disk_counters()
    prev_procs = processes()
    prev_t = time.time()
    info = static_info()
    while True:
        time.sleep(SAMPLE_EVERY)
        now = time.time()
        dt = now - prev_t

        cur_cpu = cpu_times()
        usage = []
        for (b0, t0), (b1, t1) in zip(prev_cpu, cur_cpu):
            usage.append(round(100 * (b1 - b0) / (t1 - t0), 1) if t1 > t0 else 0.0)

        cur_disk = disk_counters()
        io = []
        for d, (r, w) in cur_disk.items():
            r0, w0 = prev_disk.get(d, (r, w))
            io.append({"disk": d, "read": max(0, (r - r0) / dt), "write": max(0, (w - w0) / dt),
                       "size": int(read(f"/sys/block/{d}/size", "0").strip() or 0) * 512,
                       "model": read(f"/sys/block/{d}/device/model").strip(),
                       "ssd": read(f"/sys/block/{d}/queue/rotational").strip() == "0"})

        cur_procs = processes()
        ncpu = info["cores"] or 1
        top = []
        for pid, (name, ticks, rss, state) in cur_procs.items():
            old = prev_procs.get(pid)
            cpu = (ticks - old[1]) / CLK_TCK / dt * 100 if old else 0.0
            top.append({"pid": pid, "name": name, "cpu": round(cpu, 1), "mem": rss, "state": state})
        mem = memory()
        by_cpu = sorted(top, key=lambda p: (-p["cpu"], -p["mem"]))[:10]
        by_mem = sorted(top, key=lambda p: -p["mem"])[:10]
        for p in by_cpu + by_mem:
            p.setdefault("user", users_of(p["pid"]))
            p["mem_pct"] = round(100 * p["mem"] / mem["total"], 1) if mem["total"] else 0

        freqs, max_freq = cpu_freqs()
        load = read("/proc/loadavg").split()
        snapshot = {
            "time": now,
            "info": info,
            "uptime": float(read("/proc/uptime", "0").split()[0]),
            "cpu": {"total": usage[0], "cores": usage[1:], "freqs": freqs, "max_freq": max_freq,
                    "load": [float(x) for x in load[:3]], "ncpu": ncpu,
                    "tasks": load[3] if len(load) > 3 else ""},
            "temps": temperatures(),
            "mem": mem,
            "storage": filesystems(),
            "io": io,
            "procs": {"by_cpu": by_cpu, "by_mem": by_mem, "count": len(cur_procs)},
            "battery": battery(),
        }
        with _lock:
            _latest.clear()
            _latest.update(snapshot)
        prev_cpu, prev_disk, prev_procs, prev_t = cur_cpu, cur_disk, cur_procs, now


class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
    def _send(self, code, body, ctype="application/json"):
        data = body.encode() if isinstance(body, str) else body
        self.send_response(code)
        self.send_header("Content-Type", ctype)
        self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(data)))
        self.send_header("Cache-Control", "no-store")
        self.end_headers()
        self.wfile.write(data)

    def do_GET(self):
        path = urlparse(self.path).path
        if path == "/":
            self._send(200, PAGE, "text/html; charset=utf-8")
        elif path == "/api/stats":
            with _lock:
                data = json.dumps(_latest)
            self._send(200, data)
        else:
            self._send(404, json.dumps({"error": "not found"}))

    def log_message(self, fmt, *args):
        pass

      <div class="clock glow" id="clock">--:--:--</div>

  <div class="grid">

<script>
const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);
const esc = s => String(s ?? "").replace(/[&<>"]/g, c => ({"&":"&amp;","<":"&lt;",">":"&gt;",'"':"&quot;"}[c]));
const css = v => getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue(v).trim();

function fmt(b, d = 1) {
  const u = ["B", "K", "M", "G", "T"]; let i = 0;
  while (b >= 1024 && i < u.length - 1) { b /= 1024; i++; }
  return b.toFixed(i ? d : 0) + u[i];
}
function heat(p) { return p >= 85 ? "var(--red)" : p >= 60 ? "var(--amber)" : p >= 30 ? "var(--cyan)" : "var(--green)"; }
function tempColor(c) { return c >= 85 ? "var(--red)" : c >= 70 ? "var(--amber)" : c >= 55 ? "var(--cyan)" : "var(--green)"; }
function asciiBar(pct, width = 40) {
  const full = Math.round(pct / 100 * width);
  return "█".repeat(full) + "░".repeat(Math.max(0, width - full));
}
function uptime(s) {
  const d = Math.floor(s / 86400), h = Math.floor(s % 86400 / 3600), m = Math.floor(s % 3600 / 60);
  return (d ? d + "d " : "") + String(h).padStart(2, "0") + "h " + String(m).padStart(2, "0") + "m";
}
function barWidth(el) {  // how many characters fit
  const w = el.clientWidth || 300;
  return Math.max(8, Math.floor(w / 7.4));
}

function gauge(el, pct, label, big, sub, color) {
  const r = 70, c = 2 * Math.PI * r, arc = c * 0.75, val = arc * Math.min(100, Math.max(0, pct)) / 100;
  const ticks = Array.from({ length: 31 }, (_, i) => {
    const a = (135 + i * 9) * Math.PI / 180, r1 = 84, r2 = i % 5 ? 88 : 92;
    return `<line x1="${100 + r1 * Math.cos(a)}" y1="${100 + r1 * Math.sin(a)}" x2="${100 + r2 * Math.cos(a)}" y2="${100 + r2 * Math.sin(a)}"
      stroke="${i / 30 * 100 <= pct ? color : "#123323"}" stroke-width="2"/>`;
  }).join("");
  el.innerHTML = `<svg viewBox="0 0 200 180">
    <defs><filter id="gl${label}"><feGaussianBlur stdDeviation="3" result="b"/><feMerge><feMergeNode in="b"/><feMergeNode in="SourceGraphic"/></feMerge></filter></defs>
    ${ticks}
    <circle cx="100" cy="100" r="${r}" fill="none" stroke="#0c2a1c" stroke-width="12" stroke-dasharray="${arc} ${c}" transform="rotate(135 100 100)"/>
    <circle cx="100" cy="100" r="${r}" fill="none" stroke="${color}" stroke-width="12" stroke-dasharray="${val} ${c}"
      transform="rotate(135 100 100)" filter="url(#gl${label})" style="transition:stroke-dasharray .6s"/>
    <text x="100" y="104" text-anchor="middle" font-family="${css("--font")}" font-size="34" font-weight="800" fill="${color}" filter="url(#gl${label})">${big}</text>
    <text x="100" y="128" text-anchor="middle" font-family="${css("--font")}" font-size="11" fill="${css("--muted")}">${sub}</text>
  </svg><div class="lbl">${label}</div>`;
}

// ---------- history chart ----------
const hist = { cpu: [], ram: [], io: [] };
function drawChart() {
  const cv = $("chart"), dpr = devicePixelRatio || 1;
  const W = cv.clientWidth, H = cv.clientHeight;
  cv.width = W * dpr; cv.height = H * dpr;
  const g = cv.getContext("2d"); g.scale(dpr, dpr);
  g.strokeStyle = "#0f3d2a"; g.lineWidth = 1;
  for (let i = 0; i <= 4; i++) { const y = Math.round(H * i / 4) + .5; g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(0, y); g.lineTo(W, y); g.stroke(); }
  for (let i = 0; i <= 12; i++) { const x = Math.round(W * i / 12) + .5; g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(x, 0); g.lineTo(x, H); g.stroke(); }
  const maxIo = Math.max(1, ...hist.io);
  const line = (arr, color, scale, fill) => {
    if (arr.length < 2) return;
    g.beginPath();
    arr.forEach((v, i) => { const x = W - (arr.length - 1 - i) * (W / 59), y = H - 2 - (v / scale) * (H - 6); i ? g.lineTo(x, y) : g.moveTo(x, y); });
    g.strokeStyle = color; g.lineWidth = 1.6; g.shadowColor = color; g.shadowBlur = 8; g.stroke(); g.shadowBlur = 0;
    if (fill) { g.lineTo(W, H); g.lineTo(W - (arr.length - 1) * (W / 59), H); g.closePath(); g.fillStyle = fill; g.fill(); }
  };
  line(hist.io, css("--pink"), maxIo, null);
  line(hist.ram, css("--cyan"), 100, null);
  line(hist.cpu, css("--green"), 100, "rgba(57,255,136,.10)");
}

// ---------- render ----------
let sortBy = "cpu", last = null;
function render(d) {
  last = d;
  const i = d.info, c = d.cpu, m = d.mem;
  $("host").textContent = `// ${i.hostname}@${i.os}`;
  $("sysline").innerHTML = `kernel <b>${esc(i.kernel)}</b> · ${esc(i.arch)} · uptime <b>${uptime(d.uptime)}</b>`;
  $("cpuModel").textContent = `${i.cpu_model} · ${i.cores} threads`;

  // gauges
  const ramPct = m.total ? m.used / m.total * 100 : 0;
  const root = d.storage[0] || { used: 0, total: 1, mounts: ["?"] };
  const romPct = root.used / root.total * 100;
  const cpuTemp = (d.temps.find(t => /package|tctl|tdie/i.test(t.label)) || d.temps.find(t => t.sensor === "coretemp" || t.sensor === "k10temp") || d.temps[0]);
  gauge($("gCpu"), c.total, "CPU", Math.round(c.total) + "%", `load ${c.load[0].toFixed(2)}`, heat(c.total));
  gauge($("gRam"), ramPct, "RAM", Math.round(ramPct) + "%", `${fmt(m.used)} / ${fmt(m.total)}`, heat(ramPct));
  gauge($("gRom"), romPct, "ROM", Math.round(romPct) + "%", `${fmt(root.free)} free`, heat(romPct));
  if (cpuTemp) gauge($("gTemp"), cpuTemp.c, "TEMP", Math.round(cpuTemp.c) + "°", "celsius · cpu", tempColor(cpuTemp.c));
  else gauge($("gTemp"), 0, "TEMP", "n/a", "no sensor", css("--muted"));

  // cores
  const coresEl = $("cores"), bw = barWidth(coresEl) - 20;
  coresEl.innerHTML = c.cores.map((p, n) => `<div class="row">
      <span class="k">core${String(n).padStart(2, "0")}</span>
      <span class="ascii" style="--c:${heat(p)}">${asciiBar(p, bw)}</span>
      <span class="v">${p.toFixed(0).padStart(3)}%</span></div>`).join("");
  const avgFreq = c.freqs.filter(Boolean);
  $("cpuKv").innerHTML = [
    ["load 1/5/15", c.load.map(x => x.toFixed(2)).join(" ")],
    ["frequency", avgFreq.length ? Math.round(avgFreq.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / avgFreq.length) + " MHz" : "n/a"],
    ["max freq", c.max_freq ? c.max_freq + " MHz" : "n/a"],
    ["tasks run/total", c.tasks],
  ].map(([k, v]) => `<div><div class="k">${k}</div><div class="v">${esc(v)}</div></div>`).join("");

  // memory
  const memEl = $("mem"), mw = barWidth(memEl) - 20;
  const swapPct = m.swap_total ? m.swap_used / m.swap_total * 100 : 0;
  const cachePct = m.total ? m.cache / m.total * 100 : 0;
  $("memTotal").textContent = fmt(m.total) + " installed";
  memEl.innerHTML = [
    ["used", ramPct, `${fmt(m.used)}`],
    ["cache", cachePct, `${fmt(m.cache)}`],
    ["swap", swapPct, m.swap_total ? `${fmt(m.swap_used)}` : "none"],
  ].map(([k, p, v]) => `<div class="row"><span class="k">${k}</span>
      <span class="ascii" style="--c:${k === "cache" ? "var(--dim)" : heat(p)}">${asciiBar(p, mw)}</span><span class="v">${v}</span></div>`).join("");
  $("memKv").innerHTML = [
    ["available", fmt(m.available)], ["free (idle)", fmt(m.free)],
    ["swap total", fmt(m.swap_total)], ["processes", d.procs.count],
  ].map(([k, v]) => `<div><div class="k">${k}</div><div class="v">${esc(v)}</div></div>`).join("");

  // storage
  const stEl = $("storage"), sw = barWidth(stEl) - 22;
  stEl.innerHTML = d.storage.map(s => {
    const p = s.used / s.total * 100;
    return `<div class="row" title="${esc(s.device)} · ${esc(s.fstype)} · ${esc(s.mounts.join(", "))}">
      <span class="k">${esc(s.mounts[0])}</span>
      <span class="ascii" style="--c:${heat(p)}">${asciiBar(p, sw)}</span>
      <span class="v">${p.toFixed(0)}%</span></div>
      <div style="color:var(--muted);font-size:11px;margin:-2px 0 6px 82px">${fmt(s.used)} used / ${fmt(s.total)} · ${esc(s.fstype)}${s.mounts.length > 1 ? " · also " + esc(s.mounts.slice(1).join(", ")) : ""}</div>`;
  }).join("");
  $("ioKv").innerHTML = d.io.map(x => `<div><div class="k">${esc(x.disk)} ${x.ssd ? "ssd" : "hdd"} · ${fmt(x.size, 0)}</div>
      <div class="v">▼ ${fmt(x.read)}/s <span style="color:var(--pink)">▲ ${fmt(x.write)}/s</span></div></div>`).join("");

  // processes
  const list = sortBy === "cpu" ? d.procs.by_cpu : d.procs.by_mem;
  $("procs").innerHTML = `<tr><th class="num">PID</th><th>USER</th><th>S</th><th class="num">CPU%</th><th class="num">MEM%</th><th class="num">RES</th><th>COMMAND</th></tr>` +
    list.map(p => {
      const cls = p.cpu >= 50 ? "hot" : p.cpu >= 10 ? "warm" : "";
      return `<tr><td class="num" style="color:var(--muted)">${p.pid}</td><td>${esc(p.user)}</td><td>${esc(p.state)}</td>
        <td class="num ${cls}">${p.cpu.toFixed(1)}</td><td class="num">${p.mem_pct.toFixed(1)}</td>
        <td class="num">${fmt(p.mem)}</td><td style="color:var(--green)">${esc(p.name)}</td></tr>`;
    }).join("");

  // temps + battery
  const tEl = $("temps"), tw = barWidth(tEl) - 24;
  tEl.innerHTML = d.temps.length ? d.temps.map(t => `<div class="row">
      <span class="k" title="${esc(t.sensor)}">${esc(t.label.replace("Package id 0", "package").replace("Core ", "core"))}</span>
      <span class="ascii" style="--c:${tempColor(t.c)}">${asciiBar(Math.min(100, t.c), tw)}</span>
      <span class="v">${t.c.toFixed(0)}°C</span></div>`).join("")
    : `<div style="color:var(--muted)">no temperature sensors found</div>`;
  if (d.battery) {
    const b = d.battery, icon = b.status === "Charging" ? "⚡" : b.status === "Full" ? "■" : "▮";
    $("battery").innerHTML = `<span style="color:${heat(100 - b.percent)}">BAT ${icon} ${b.percent}%</span> ${esc(b.status.toLowerCase())}`;
  }

  // history
  const ioTotal = d.io.reduce((s, x) => s + x.read + x.write, 0);
  hist.cpu.push(c.total); hist.ram.push(ramPct); hist.io.push(ioTotal);
  for (const k in hist) if (hist[k].length > 60) hist[k].shift();
  drawChart();
}

let fails = 0;
async function tick() {
  try {
    const r = await fetch("/api/stats");
    const d = await r.json();
    if (d.cpu) { render(d); fails = 0; }
  } catch (e) {
    if (++fails > 2) $("host").innerHTML = `<span class="err">// CONNECTION LOST - is sysmon.py still running?</span>`;
  }
}
document.querySelectorAll(".tab").forEach(t => t.onclick = () => {
  document.querySelectorAll(".tab").forEach(x => x.classList.remove("on"));
  t.classList.add("on"); sortBy = t.dataset.s; if (last) render(last);
});
setInterval(() => { $("clock").textContent = new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], { hour12: false }); }, 250);
addEventListener("resize", () => last && render(last));

// ---------- matrix rain ----------
let rainOn = true;
try { rainOn = localStorage.getItem("sysmon-rain") !== "off"; } catch (e) {}
(function rain() {
  const cv = $("rain"), g = cv.getContext("2d"), chars = "01アイウエオカキクケコサシスセソタチツテト<>/{}[]$#%&*+=";
  let cols = [], fs = 14;
  const size = () => { cv.width = innerWidth; cv.height = innerHeight; cols = Array(Math.ceil(innerWidth / fs)).fill(0).map(() => Math.random() * -50); };
  size(); addEventListener("resize", size);
  setInterval(() => {
    if (!rainOn) { g.clearRect(0, 0, cv.width, cv.height); return; }
    g.fillStyle = "rgba(4,7,10,.12)"; g.fillRect(0, 0, cv.width, cv.height);
    g.fillStyle = "#39ff88"; g.font = fs + "px monospace";
    cols.forEach((y, i) => {
      g.fillText(chars[Math.random() * chars.length | 0], i * fs, y * fs);
      cols[i] = y * fs > cv.height && Math.random() > .975 ? 0 : y + 1;
    });
  }, 60);
})();
$("rainBtn").onclick = () => { rainOn = !rainOn; try { localStorage.setItem("sysmon-rain", rainOn ? "on" : "off"); } catch (e) {} };

// ---------- boot sequence ----------
(async function boot() {
  const lines = [
    "SYSMON BIOS v4.2.0 (c) 1997-2026 h4x0r industries",
    "probing /proc/stat .................. [ OK ]",
    "probing /proc/meminfo ............... [ OK ]",
    "mounting /sys/class/hwmon ........... [ OK ]",
    "calibrating flux capacitor .......... [ OK ]",
    "establishing uplink to 127.0.0.1 .... [ OK ]",
    "",
    "> ACCESS GRANTED_",
  ];
  const el = $("boot");
  let skip = false;
  el.onclick = () => skip = true;
  for (const l of lines) {
    if (skip) break;
    el.textContent += l + "\n";
    await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 110));
  }
  setTimeout(() => el.classList.add("done"), skip ? 0 : 350);
})();

tick();
setInterval(tick, 1000);
</script>
</body>
</html>
"""


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Geeky live CPU / RAM / storage monitor in your browser")
    ap.add_argument("--host", default="127.0.0.1", help="address to listen on (default: local only)")
    ap.add_argument("--port", type=int, default=8769)
    args = ap.parse_args()
    threading.Thread(target=sampler, daemon=True).start()
    server = ThreadingHTTPServer((args.host, args.port), Handler)
    print(f"SYSMON online at http://{args.host}:{args.port}  (Ctrl+C to stop)")
    try:
        server.serve_forever()
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        print("\nSYSMON offline.")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

================================================================================
FILE: traffic.py

#!/usr/bin/env python3
# SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-or-later
# Copyright (C) 2026 Denis
"""Traffic - watches the network connections of *your* user session.

Used by collective.py. Can also be run alone to print a quick summary:
    python3 traffic.py

What it shows (no root / sudo needed):
  * every connection your own programs have open: which program, to which
    address and port, incoming or outgoing, and how much data went each way
  * which ports your programs are listening on (waiting for incoming traffic)
  * live download / upload speed of your programs and of the whole computer
  * per-program totals since Collective Scanner was started

What it does NOT do: it never reads the content of your traffic and never
sends anything anywhere. Addresses are turned into names with your computer's
normal reverse-DNS lookup only.

Uses `ss` (part of iproute, installed on Fedora by default) and /proc/net/dev.
Python standard library only.
"""

import ipaddress
import os
import re
import socket
import subprocess
import threading
import time
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor

MY_UID = os.getuid()
HISTORY = 120          # seconds of speed history kept for the charts
SOCK_EVERY = 2.0       # how often connections are re-read
IFACE_EVERY = 1.0      # how often the interface counters are re-read

# Friendly names for common ports (falls back to /etc/services).
SERVICES = {
    20: "FTP data", 21: "FTP", 22: "SSH", 25: "Mail (SMTP)", 53: "DNS", 67: "DHCP", 68: "DHCP",
    80: "Web (HTTP)", 110: "Mail (POP3)", 123: "Time (NTP)", 143: "Mail (IMAP)",
    443: "Secure web (HTTPS)", 465: "Mail (SMTPS)", 587: "Mail (submission)", 631: "Printing",
    853: "Secure DNS", 993: "Mail (IMAPS)", 995: "Mail (POP3S)", 1900: "UPnP / SSDP",
    3478: "Calls (STUN)", 3702: "Network discovery", 5222: "Chat (XMPP)", 5228: "Google Play / FCM",
    5353: "Local discovery (mDNS)", 5355: "Local names (LLMNR)", 8080: "Web (alt)", 8443: "Secure web (alt)",
    19302: "Google Meet / STUN", 4070: "Spotify", 27036: "Steam",
}

_lock = threading.Lock()
_started = time.time()
_iface_hist = []       # [{"t", "rx", "tx"}]   bytes/s for the whole computer
_user_hist = []        # [{"t", "rx", "tx"}]   bytes/s for your TCP connections
_ifaces = {}           # name -> {"rx", "tx", "rx_rate", "tx_rate"}
_conns = []            # latest list of your connections
_listening = []        # latest list of your listening sockets
_others = 0            # connections owned by system / other users (count only)
_apps = {}             # program -> {"sent", "recv", "first", "last"}
_session = {"sent": 0, "recv": 0}
_seen_bytes = {}       # socket key -> (sent, recv) from the previous sample
_first_sample = True

_names = {}            # ip -> reverse-DNS name ("" if none)
_name_jobs = set()
_pool = ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=4)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- helpers

def service_name(port, proto="tcp"):
    if port in SERVICES:
        return SERVICES[port]
    try:
        return socket.getservbyport(port, proto).upper()
    except (OSError, OverflowError):
        return ""


def split_addr(text):
    """'192.168.1.2:443' / '[::1]:631' / '127.0.0.53%lo:53' / '*:*' -> (ip, port)."""
    host, _, port = text.rpartition(":")
    host = host.strip("[]").split("%")[0]
    if host.startswith("::ffff:") and "." in host:
        host = host[7:]
    return host, (int(port) if port.isdigit() else None)


def addr_kind(ip):
    try:
        a = ipaddress.ip_address(ip)
    except ValueError:
        return "any"
    if a.is_loopback:
        return "this computer"
    if a.is_multicast:
        return "broadcast"
    if a.is_unspecified:
        return "any"
    if a.is_private or a.is_link_local:
        return "home network"
    return "internet"


def _resolve(ip):
    try:
        name = socket.gethostbyaddr(ip)[0]
    except (OSError, UnicodeError):
        name = ""
    with _lock:
        _names[ip] = name
        _name_jobs.discard(ip)


def name_for(ip):
    """Reverse-DNS name, looked up in the background so nothing ever waits."""
    if ip in _names:
        return _names[ip]
    if ip not in _name_jobs and addr_kind(ip) in ("internet", "home network"):
        _name_jobs.add(ip)
        _pool.submit(_resolve, ip)
    return ""


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- reading

_USERS_RE = re.compile(r'\("([^"]*)",pid=(\d+)')


def read_sockets():
    """All TCP/UDP sockets from `ss`, with byte counters for TCP."""
    try:
        out = subprocess.run(["ss", "-tuanpeiH"], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=5).stdout
    except (OSError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired):
        return []
    socks = []
    for line in out.splitlines():
        if not line.strip():
            continue
        if line[0].isspace():                      # detail line for the socket above
            if socks:
                for key in ("bytes_sent", "bytes_received"):
                    m = re.search(key + r":(\d+)", line)
                    if m:
                        socks[-1][key] = int(m.group(1))
            continue
        parts = line.split()
        if len(parts) < 6:
            continue
        netid, state, local, peer = parts[0], parts[1], parts[4], parts[5]
        rest = " ".join(parts[6:])
        uid = re.search(r"\buid:(\d+)", rest)
        ino = re.search(r"\bino:(\d+)", rest)
        users = _USERS_RE.findall(rest)
        lip, lport = split_addr(local)
        rip, rport = split_addr(peer)
        socks.append({
            "proto": netid, "state": state, "lip": lip, "lport": lport, "rip": rip, "rport": rport,
            "uid": int(uid.group(1)) if uid else 0, "ino": ino.group(1) if ino else "",
            "app": users[0][0] if users else "", "pid": int(users[0][1]) if users else None,
        })
    return socks


def read_ifaces():
    data = {}
    try:
        with open("/proc/net/dev") as f:
            lines = f.readlines()[2:]
    except OSError:
        return data
    for line in lines:
        name, _, nums = line.partition(":")
        name, nums = name.strip(), nums.split()
        if name == "lo" or len(nums) < 9:
            continue
        data[name] = {"rx": int(nums[0]), "tx": int(nums[8])}
    return data


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- sampling

def _sample_sockets(dt):
    global _first_sample, _others
    socks = read_sockets()
    mine = [s for s in socks if s["uid"] == MY_UID]
    listen_ports = {(s["proto"], s["lport"]) for s in mine if s["state"] in ("LISTEN", "UNCONN") and s["rport"] is None}
    listen_ports |= {(s["proto"], s["lport"]) for s in socks if s["state"] == "LISTEN"}

    conns, listening, alive = [], [], {}
    in_rate = out_rate = 0.0
    for s in mine:
        if s["rport"] is None:                     # listening / not connected
            if s["state"] in ("LISTEN", "UNCONN"):
                listening.append({
                    "proto": s["proto"], "ip": s["lip"], "port": s["lport"], "app": s["app"], "pid": s["pid"],
                    "service": service_name(s["lport"], s["proto"]),
                    "open_to": "only this computer" if addr_kind(s["lip"]) == "this computer" else "the network",
                })
            continue
        key = f'{s["proto"]}|{s["ino"]}|{s["lip"]}:{s["lport"]}|{s["rip"]}:{s["rport"]}'
        sent, recv = s.get("bytes_sent", 0), s.get("bytes_received", 0)
        prev = _seen_bytes.get(key)
        if prev is None:
            prev = (sent, recv) if _first_sample else (0, 0)   # count new connections from zero
        d_sent, d_recv = max(0, sent - prev[0]), max(0, recv - prev[1])
        alive[key] = (sent, recv)
        incoming = (s["proto"], s["lport"]) in listen_ports and s["proto"] == "tcp"
        app = s["app"] or "unknown"
        kind = addr_kind(s["rip"])
        if kind != "this computer":                # local-only chatter is not network traffic
            in_rate += d_recv / dt
            out_rate += d_sent / dt
            a = _apps.setdefault(app, {"sent": 0, "recv": 0, "first": time.time(), "last": time.time()})
            a["sent"] += d_sent
            a["recv"] += d_recv
            if d_sent or d_recv:
                a["last"] = time.time()
            _session["sent"] += d_sent
            _session["recv"] += d_recv
        port = s["lport"] if incoming else s["rport"]
        conns.append({
            "proto": s["proto"], "state": s["state"], "direction": "in" if incoming else "out",
            "local": s["lip"], "lport": s["lport"], "remote": s["rip"], "rport": s["rport"],
            "name": name_for(s["rip"]), "kind": kind, "service": service_name(port, s["proto"]),
            "app": app, "pid": s["pid"], "sent": sent, "recv": recv,
            "in_rate": d_recv / dt, "out_rate": d_sent / dt,
        })
    _seen_bytes.clear()
    _seen_bytes.update(alive)
    _first_sample = False
    others = sum(1 for s in socks if s["uid"] != MY_UID and s["rport"] is not None
                 and s["state"] not in ("TIME-WAIT", "CLOSE-WAIT", "FIN-WAIT-1", "FIN-WAIT-2", "LAST-ACK", "CLOSING")
                 and addr_kind(s["rip"]) != "this computer")
    return conns, listening, others, in_rate, out_rate


def sampler():
    """Background thread: keeps the live numbers fresh. Start it once."""
    global _conns, _listening, _others
    prev_if, prev_t = read_ifaces(), time.time()
    last_sock = 0.0
    while True:
        time.sleep(IFACE_EVERY)
        now = time.time()
        cur_if = read_ifaces()
        dt = max(1e-3, now - prev_t)
        rx = tx = 0.0
        ifaces = {}
        for name, v in cur_if.items():
            p = prev_if.get(name, v)
            r, t = max(0, v["rx"] - p["rx"]) / dt, max(0, v["tx"] - p["tx"]) / dt
            ifaces[name] = {"rx": v["rx"], "tx": v["tx"], "rx_rate": r, "tx_rate": t}
            rx, tx = rx + r, tx + t
        prev_if, prev_t = cur_if, now

        sock_data = None
        if now - last_sock >= SOCK_EVERY:
            sdt = (now - last_sock) if last_sock else SOCK_EVERY
            last_sock = now
            try:
                sock_data = _sample_sockets(sdt)
            except Exception:  # never let one bad read stop the monitor
                sock_data = None

        with _lock:
            _ifaces.clear()
            _ifaces.update(ifaces)
            _iface_hist.append({"t": now, "rx": rx, "tx": tx})
            del _iface_hist[:-HISTORY]
            if sock_data:
                _conns, _listening, _others, in_r, out_r = sock_data
                _user_hist.append({"t": now, "rx": in_r, "tx": out_r})
                del _user_hist[:-HISTORY // 2]


_started_thread = False


def start():
    global _started_thread
    if not _started_thread:
        _started_thread = True
        threading.Thread(target=sampler, daemon=True).start()


def snapshot():
    """Everything the web page needs, in one JSON-friendly dict."""
    with _lock:
        conns = [dict(c, name=_names.get(c["remote"], c["name"])) for c in _conns]
        apps = {}
        for c in conns:
            if c["kind"] == "this computer":
                continue
            a = apps.setdefault(c["app"], {"app": c["app"], "connections": 0, "in_rate": 0, "out_rate": 0, "remotes": set()})
            a["connections"] += 1
            a["in_rate"] += c["in_rate"]
            a["out_rate"] += c["out_rate"]
            a["remotes"].add(c["name"] or c["remote"])
        for name, tot in _apps.items():
            a = apps.setdefault(name, {"app": name, "connections": 0, "in_rate": 0, "out_rate": 0, "remotes": set()})
            a.update(sent=tot["sent"], recv=tot["recv"], last=tot["last"])
        app_list = []
        for a in apps.values():
            a.setdefault("sent", 0)
            a.setdefault("recv", 0)
            a.setdefault("last", None)
            a["remotes"] = sorted(a["remotes"])[:6]
            app_list.append(a)
        app_list.sort(key=lambda a: (a["sent"] + a["recv"], a["connections"]), reverse=True)
        return {
            "time": time.time(), "started": _started, "user": _user_name(),
            "iface_hist": list(_iface_hist), "user_hist": list(_user_hist), "ifaces": dict(_ifaces),
            "connections": conns, "listening": list(_listening), "apps": app_list,
            "session": dict(_session), "other_connections": _others,
        }


def _user_name():
    try:
        import pwd
        return pwd.getpwuid(MY_UID).pw_name
    except (ImportError, KeyError):
        return str(MY_UID)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    start()
    print("Watching your connections for 5 seconds…")
    time.sleep(5)
    s = snapshot()
    print(f"user {s['user']}: {len(s['connections'])} connections, {len(s['listening'])} listening, "
          f"{s['other_connections']} belong to the system/other users")
    for c in sorted(s["connections"], key=lambda c: c["recv"] + c["sent"], reverse=True)[:15]:
        arrow = "<-" if c["direction"] == "in" else "->"
        print(f"  {c['app'][:16]:16} {arrow} {(c['name'] or c['remote'])[:40]:40} :{c['rport']:<5} "
              f"{c['service'][:18]:18} in {c['recv']:>10} B  out {c['sent']:>10} B")
