#!/usr/bin/env python3
# SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-or-later
# Copyright (C) 2026 Denis
"""Wi-Fi Nearby - a tiny local web app that shows the wireless networks around you.

Run:   python3 wifiscan.py            then open http://127.0.0.1:8766
Opts:  --port 9000   --host 127.0.0.1

Uses NetworkManager's `nmcli` (installed on Fedora by default).
Python standard library only.
"""

import argparse
import json
import re
import subprocess
from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer
from urllib.parse import parse_qs, urlparse

FIELDS = ["IN-USE", "SSID", "BSSID", "SIGNAL", "CHAN", "FREQ", "SECURITY", "RATE", "DEVICE"]


def split_nmcli(line):
    """Split one line of `nmcli -t` output (':' separated, '\\:' escaped)."""
    parts = re.split(r"(?<!\\):", line)
    return [p.replace("\\:", ":").replace("\\\\", "\\") for p in parts]


def scan(rescan=False):
    cmd = ["nmcli", "-t", "-f", ",".join(FIELDS), "dev", "wifi", "list",
           "--rescan", "yes" if rescan else "auto"]
    try:
        out = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30)
    except FileNotFoundError:
        raise RuntimeError("nmcli was not found. Install NetworkManager: sudo dnf install NetworkManager")
    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
        raise RuntimeError("The Wi-Fi scan took too long. Please try again.")
    if out.returncode != 0:
        msg = out.stderr.strip() or "Wi-Fi scan failed."
        raise RuntimeError(msg)

    access_points = []
    for line in out.stdout.splitlines():
        if not line.strip():
            continue
        vals = split_nmcli(line)
        if len(vals) < len(FIELDS):
            continue
        row = dict(zip(FIELDS, vals))
        try:
            freq = int(row["FREQ"].split()[0])
        except (ValueError, IndexError):
            freq = 0
        access_points.append({
            "connected": row["IN-USE"].strip() == "*",
            "ssid": row["SSID"],
            "bssid": row["BSSID"],
            "signal": int(row["SIGNAL"] or 0),
            "channel": row["CHAN"],
            "freq": freq,
            "band": "6 GHz" if freq >= 5925 else "5 GHz" if freq >= 4900 else "2.4 GHz",
            "security": row["SECURITY"].strip() or "Open",
            "rate": row["RATE"],
            "device": row["DEVICE"],
        })

    # Group access points that broadcast the same network name.
    networks = {}
    for ap in access_points:
        key = ap["ssid"] or f"__hidden__{ap['bssid']}"
        net = networks.setdefault(key, {
            "ssid": ap["ssid"], "hidden": not ap["ssid"],
            "access_points": [], "connected": False,
        })
        net["access_points"].append(ap)
        net["connected"] |= ap["connected"]

    result = []
    for net in networks.values():
        aps = sorted(net["access_points"], key=lambda a: a["signal"], reverse=True)
        best = aps[0]
        result.append({
            "ssid": net["ssid"],
            "hidden": net["hidden"],
            "connected": net["connected"],
            "signal": best["signal"],
            "security": best["security"],
            "bands": sorted({a["band"] for a in aps}),
            "access_points": aps,
        })
    result.sort(key=lambda n: (not n["connected"], -n["signal"]))
    return result


def wifi_status():
    out = subprocess.run(["nmcli", "-t", "-f", "DEVICE,TYPE,STATE,CONNECTION", "dev"],
                         capture_output=True, text=True)
    devices = []
    for line in out.stdout.splitlines():
        vals = split_nmcli(line)
        if len(vals) >= 4 and vals[1] == "wifi":
            devices.append({"device": vals[0], "state": vals[2], "connection": vals[3]})
    return devices


class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
    def _send(self, code, body, ctype):
        data = body.encode() if isinstance(body, str) else body
        self.send_response(code)
        self.send_header("Content-Type", ctype)
        self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(data)))
        self.end_headers()
        self.wfile.write(data)

    def do_GET(self):
        url = urlparse(self.path)
        q = parse_qs(url.query)
        try:
            if url.path == "/":
                self._send(200, PAGE, "text/html; charset=utf-8")
            elif url.path == "/api/scan":
                rescan = q.get("rescan", ["0"])[0] == "1"
                data = {"networks": scan(rescan), "devices": wifi_status()}
                self._send(200, json.dumps(data), "application/json")
            else:
                self._send(404, json.dumps({"error": "not found"}), "application/json")
        except Exception as e:
            self._send(500, json.dumps({"error": str(e)}), "application/json")

    def log_message(self, fmt, *args):
        pass

<script>
const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);
const esc = s => String(s).replace(/[&<>"]/g, c => ({"&":"&amp;","<":"&lt;",">":"&gt;",'"':"&quot;"}[c]));

function quality(sig) {
  if (sig >= 75) return { label: "Excellent", emoji: "🤩", color: "var(--mint)", bars: 4, hint: "Super strong. Great for video calls and streaming." };
  if (sig >= 55) return { label: "Good", emoji: "😊", color: "var(--sky)", bars: 3, hint: "Strong and reliable for everyday use." };
  if (sig >= 35) return { label: "Okay", emoji: "🙂", color: "var(--sun)", bars: 2, hint: "Works, but may be slower. Try moving closer." };
  return { label: "Weak", emoji: "😕", color: "var(--coral)", bars: 1, hint: "Far away or blocked. Expect drop-outs." };
}

function securityLabel(sec) {
  if (sec === "Open") return `<span class="badge b-open">🔓 Open</span>`;
  const nice = sec.replace("WPA1", "WPA").split(" ").join(" / ");
  return `<span class="badge b-lock" title="${esc(sec)}">🔒 Locked · ${esc(nice)}</span>`;
}

function render(data) {
  const nets = data.networks;
  const strong = nets.filter(n => n.signal >= 55).length;
  const open = nets.filter(n => n.security === "Open").length;
  const aps = nets.reduce((s, n) => s + n.access_points.length, 0);
  $("summary").innerHTML = `
    <div class="pill a"><b>${nets.length}</b>networks found</div>
    <div class="pill b"><b>${strong}</b>with a strong signal</div>
    <div class="pill c"><b>${aps}</b>access points</div>
    <div class="pill d"><b>${open}</b>open (no password)</div>`;

  const dev = data.devices[0];
  if (dev) {
    $("status").hidden = false;
    $("status").innerHTML = dev.state === "connected"
      ? `✅ Your Wi-Fi (<b>${esc(dev.device)}</b>) is connected to <b>${esc(dev.connection)}</b>.`
      : `💡 Your Wi-Fi (<b>${esc(dev.device)}</b>) is <b>${esc(dev.state)}</b>. You're probably online through a cable. Scanning still works fine.`;
  }

  if (!nets.length) {
    $("list").innerHTML = `<div class="msg">🙈 No networks found. Is Wi-Fi switched on?</div>`;
    return;
  }

  $("list").innerHTML = `<div class="grid">${nets.map(n => {
    const q = quality(n.signal);
    const bars = [1,2,3,4].map(i => `<i class="${i <= q.bars ? "on" : ""}"></i>`).join("");
    const rows = n.access_points.map(a => `<tr>
        <td>${a.connected ? "⭐ " : ""}${esc(a.bssid)}</td><td>${a.signal}%</td>
        <td>${esc(a.band)} · ch ${esc(a.channel)}</td><td>${esc(a.rate)}</td></tr>`).join("");
    return `<div class="net ${n.connected ? "connected" : ""}" style="--c:${q.color}">
      <div class="top">
        <div>
          <div class="name ${n.hidden ? "hidden" : ""}">${n.hidden ? "Hidden network" : esc(n.ssid)}</div>
          ${n.connected ? `<span class="badge b-conn">✔ You're connected</span>` : ""}
          ${securityLabel(n.security)}
          ${n.bands.map(b => `<span class="badge b-band">📡 ${esc(b)}</span>`).join("")}
        </div>
        <div class="bars" title="Signal ${n.signal}%">${bars}</div>
      </div>
      <div class="meter"><span style="width:${n.signal}%"></span></div>
      <div class="quality"><span>${q.emoji} ${q.label}</span><small>${n.signal}%</small></div>
      <div class="hint">${q.hint}</div>
      <details>
        <summary>Details · ${n.access_points.length} access point${n.access_points.length > 1 ? "s" : ""}</summary>
        <div class="tablewrap"><table>
          <tr><th>Address</th><th>Signal</th><th>Band</th><th>Max speed</th></tr>${rows}
        </table></div>
      </details>
    </div>`;
  }).join("")}</div>`;
}

let busy = false;
async function load(rescan = false) {
  if (busy) return;
  busy = true;
  $("scanBtn").disabled = true;
  $("scanBtn").innerHTML = `<span class="spin">🔄</span> Scanning…`;
  try {
    const res = await fetch("/api/scan" + (rescan ? "?rescan=1" : ""));
    const data = await res.json();
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(data.error);
    // Keep "Details" panels open across refreshes
    const open = [...document.querySelectorAll("details[open]")].map(d => d.closest(".net").querySelector(".name").textContent);
    render(data);
    document.querySelectorAll(".net").forEach(el => {
      if (open.includes(el.querySelector(".name").textContent)) el.querySelector("details").open = true;
    });
    $("updated").textContent = "Last updated " + new Date().toLocaleTimeString();
  } catch (e) {
    $("list").innerHTML = `<div class="msg err">😬 ${esc(e.message)}</div>`;
  } finally {
    busy = false;
    $("scanBtn").disabled = false;
    $("scanBtn").innerHTML = "🔄 Scan again";
  }
}

$("scanBtn").onclick = () => load(true);
setInterval(() => { if ($("auto").checked) load(false); }, 20000);
load(false);
</script>
</body>
</html>
"""


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Show nearby Wi-Fi networks in a web page")
    ap.add_argument("--host", default="127.0.0.1", help="address to listen on (default: local only)")
    ap.add_argument("--port", type=int, default=8766)
    args = ap.parse_args()
    server = ThreadingHTTPServer((args.host, args.port), Handler)
    print(f"Wi-Fi Nearby running at http://{args.host}:{args.port}  (Ctrl+C to stop)")
    try:
        server.serve_forever()
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        print("\nStopped.")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
